========== FILE: C:\Users\Jayden\WebstormProjects\Vyte\orbyteflow\src\components\common\VyteDropdown.tsx ==========

// src/components/common/VyteDropdown.tsx
import React, { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import { createPortal } from 'react-dom';
import { ChevronDown, Check } from 'lucide-react';

export interface VyteDropdownOption<T extends string | number = string> {
    label: string;
    value: T;
    badge?: string;
}

export interface VyteDropdownProps<T extends string | number = string> {
    value: T;
    options: VyteDropdownOption<T>[];
    onChange: (value: T) => void;
    prefix?: string;
    icon?: React.ReactNode;
    variant?: 'light' | 'soft' | 'dark' | 'purple';
    size?: 'sm' | 'md';
    className?: string;
    disabled?: boolean;
}

export function VyteDropdown<T extends string | number = string>({
                                                                     value,
                                                                     options,
                                                                     onChange,
                                                                     prefix,
                                                                     icon,
                                                                     variant = 'light',
                                                                     size = 'md',
                                                                     className = '',
                                                                     disabled = false,
                                                                 }: VyteDropdownProps<T>) {
    const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);
    const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
    const menuRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
    const [menuPosition, setMenuPosition] = useState<React.CSSProperties>({});
    const [openUpward, setOpenUpward] = useState(false);

    const selectedOption = options.find((opt) => opt.value === value) || options[0];

    // Compute dynamic coordinates so the menu overlays over tables and scroll containers
    const updatePosition = () => {
        if (!containerRef.current) return;
        const rect = containerRef.current.getBoundingClientRect();
        const estimatedMenuHeight = 224; // max-h-56

        const spaceBelow = window.innerHeight - rect.bottom;
        const shouldOpenUp = spaceBelow < estimatedMenuHeight && rect.top > estimatedMenuHeight;

        setOpenUpward(shouldOpenUp);
        setMenuPosition({
            position: 'fixed',
            left: `${rect.left}px`,
            width: `${rect.width}px`,
            top: shouldOpenUp ? undefined : `${rect.bottom - 1}px`,
            bottom: shouldOpenUp ? `${window.innerHeight - rect.top - 1}px` : undefined,
            zIndex: 99999,
        });
    };

    useEffect(() => {
        if (!isOpen) return;

        updatePosition();

        const handleScrollOrResize = () => {
            updatePosition();
        };

        window.addEventListener('resize', handleScrollOrResize);
        window.addEventListener('scroll', handleScrollOrResize, true);

        return () => {
            window.removeEventListener('resize', handleScrollOrResize);
            window.removeEventListener('scroll', handleScrollOrResize, true);
        };
    }, [isOpen]);

    // Close on click outside (checking both trigger button and portaled menu)
    useEffect(() => {
        const handleClickOutside = (e: MouseEvent) => {
            const target = e.target as Node;
            const clickedInsideTrigger = containerRef.current?.contains(target);
            const clickedInsideMenu = menuRef.current?.contains(target);

            if (!clickedInsideTrigger && !clickedInsideMenu) {
                setIsOpen(false);
            }
        };

        const handleEscape = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
            if (e.key === 'Escape') setIsOpen(false);
        };

        document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
        document.addEventListener('keydown', handleEscape);
        return () => {
            document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
            document.removeEventListener('keydown', handleEscape);
        };
    }, []);

    const sizeStyles =
        size === 'sm'
            ? 'px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs rounded-lg'
            : 'px-3.5 py-2 text-xs rounded-xl';

    const openTopRadius =
        size === 'sm' ? 'rounded-t-lg rounded-b-none' : 'rounded-t-xl rounded-b-none';
    const openBottomRadius =
        size === 'sm' ? 'rounded-b-lg rounded-t-none' : 'rounded-b-xl rounded-t-none';

    const getTriggerTheme = () => {
        if (variant === 'dark') {
            return isOpen
                ? 'bg-[#1E242B] border-[#2BB656] text-emerald-400 border-b-transparent shadow-lg'
                : 'bg-[#1E242B] border-slate-700/80 hover:border-[#2BB656]/60 text-emerald-400';
        }
        if (variant === 'purple') {
            return isOpen
                ? 'bg-white border-purple-400 text-purple-900 border-b-transparent shadow-md'
                : 'bg-purple-50/80 border-purple-200 hover:border-purple-300 text-purple-800';
        }
        if (variant === 'soft') {
            return isOpen
                ? 'bg-white border-[#2BB656] text-slate-900 border-b-transparent shadow-md'
                : 'bg-slate-50 border-slate-200 hover:border-slate-300 text-slate-800';
        }
        return isOpen
            ? 'bg-white border-[#2BB656] text-slate-900 border-b-transparent shadow-md'
            : 'bg-white border-slate-300 hover:border-slate-400 text-slate-900';
    };

    const getMenuTheme = () => {
        if (variant === 'dark') {
            return 'bg-[#1E242B] border-[#2BB656] text-slate-200 divide-slate-800/80 shadow-2xl';
        }
        if (variant === 'purple') {
            return 'bg-white border-purple-400 text-slate-800 divide-purple-100 shadow-2xl';
        }
        return 'bg-white border-[#2BB656] text-slate-800 divide-slate-100 shadow-2xl';
    };

    const getOptionTheme = (isSelected: boolean) => {
        if (variant === 'dark') {
            return isSelected
                ? 'bg-[#2BB656]/20 text-emerald-400 font-bold'
                : 'text-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-800/90 hover:text-white font-medium';
        }
        if (variant === 'purple') {
            return isSelected
                ? 'bg-purple-50 text-purple-900 font-bold'
                : 'text-slate-700 hover:bg-purple-50/50 hover:text-purple-900 font-medium';
        }
        return isSelected
            ? 'bg-[#EBFDF2] text-emerald-900 font-bold'
            : 'text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50 hover:text-slate-900 font-medium';
    };

    return (
        <div ref={containerRef} className={`relative select-none ${className}`}>
            <button
                type="button"
                disabled={disabled}
                onClick={() => {
                    if (!isOpen) updatePosition();
                    setIsOpen((prev) => !prev);
                }}
                className={`w-full flex items-center justify-between gap-2 border font-semibold transition-colors cursor-pointer focus:outline-none ${sizeStyles} ${
                    isOpen ? (openUpward ? openBottomRadius : openTopRadius) : ''
                } ${getTriggerTheme()} ${disabled ? 'opacity-50 cursor-not-allowed' : ''}`}
            >
                <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 min-w-0 truncate">
                    {icon && <span className="shrink-0">{icon}</span>}
                    {prefix && (
                        <span
                            className={`font-semibold shrink-0 ${
                                variant === 'dark' ? 'text-slate-400' : 'text-slate-500'
                            }`}
                        >
                            {prefix}
                        </span>
                    )}
                    <span className="truncate font-bold">{selectedOption?.label}</span>
                </span>
                <ChevronDown
                    className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0 transition-transform duration-200 ${
                        isOpen ? 'rotate-180' : ''
                    } ${
                        variant === 'dark'
                            ? 'text-emerald-400'
                            : variant === 'purple'
                                ? 'text-purple-600'
                                : 'text-slate-400'
                    }`}
                />
            </button>

            {/* Render options menu in document.body to break free from table overflow boundaries */}
            {isOpen &&
                createPortal(
                    <div
                        ref={menuRef}
                        style={menuPosition}
                        className={`border overflow-hidden ${
                            openUpward
                                ? size === 'sm'
                                    ? 'rounded-t-lg border-b-0'
                                    : 'rounded-t-xl border-b-0'
                                : openBottomRadius
                        } ${getMenuTheme()}`}
                    >
                        <div
                            className={`border-t ${
                                variant === 'dark'
                                    ? 'border-slate-700/70'
                                    : variant === 'purple'
                                        ? 'border-purple-100'
                                        : 'border-slate-100'
                            }`}
                        />
                        <div className="max-h-56 overflow-y-auto py-1">
                            {options.map((opt) => {
                                const isSelected = opt.value === value;
                                return (
                                    <button
                                        key={String(opt.value)}
                                        type="button"
                                        onClick={() => {
                                            onChange(opt.value);
                                            setIsOpen(false);
                                        }}
                                        className={`w-full px-3.5 py-2 text-left text-xs flex items-center justify-between gap-2 transition-colors cursor-pointer ${getOptionTheme(
                                            isSelected
                                        )}`}
                                    >
                                        <span className="truncate">{opt.label}</span>
                                        {isSelected && (
                                            <Check
                                                className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0 ${
                                                    variant === 'dark'
                                                        ? 'text-emerald-400'
                                                        : variant === 'purple'
                                                            ? 'text-purple-600'
                                                            : 'text-[#2BB656]'
                                                }`}
                                            />
                                        )}
                                    </button>
                                );
                            })}
                        </div>
                    </div>,
                    document.body
                )}
        </div>
    );
}

========== FILE: C:\Users\Jayden\WebstormProjects\Vyte\orbyteflow\src\components\common\VyteLogo.tsx ==========

// src/components/common/VyteLogo.tsx
import React from 'react';
import startAnimData from '../start_anim.json';

interface VyteLogoProps {
    variant?: 'dark-text' | 'light-text';
    showSubtitle?: boolean;
    iconOnly?: boolean;
    className?: string;
}

/** @Composable */
export const VyteLogo: React.FC<VyteLogoProps> = ({
                                                      variant = 'dark-text',
                                                      showSubtitle = true,
                                                      iconOnly = false,
                                                      className = 'h-10 w-auto',
                                                  }) => {
    const { glyphs, subtitle } = startAnimData.logoGeometry;
    const primaryInk = variant === 'light-text' ? '#FFFFFF' : '#0B0C0E';
    const vyteGreen = '#2BB656';

    if (iconOnly) {
        return (
            <svg viewBox="115 100 260 270" className={className} aria-label="Vyte">
                <path d={glyphs.V_leftLeg.d} fill={primaryInk} />
                <path d={glyphs.V_rightGreenSlash.d} fill={vyteGreen} />
            </svg>
        );
    }

    return (
        <svg
            viewBox={showSubtitle ? '85 75 760 440' : '85 95 760 345'}
            preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet"
            className={className}
            aria-label="Vyte Inventory Management"
        >
            {/* V */}
            <path d={glyphs.V_leftLeg.d} fill={primaryInk} />
            <path d={glyphs.V_rightGreenSlash.d} fill={vyteGreen} />

            {/* y */}
            <path d={glyphs.y_leftFork.d} fill={primaryInk} />
            <path d={glyphs.y_descender.d} fill={primaryInk} />
            <circle
                cx={glyphs.y_topGreenDot.cx}
                cy={glyphs.y_topGreenDot.cy}
                r={glyphs.y_topGreenDot.r}
                fill={vyteGreen}
            />
            <circle
                cx={glyphs.y_bottomGreenDot.cx}
                cy={glyphs.y_bottomGreenDot.cy}
                r={glyphs.y_bottomGreenDot.r}
                fill={vyteGreen}
            />

            {/* t */}
            <path d={glyphs.t_body.d} fill={primaryInk} />

            {/* e */}
            <path d={glyphs.e_body.d} fill={primaryInk} fillRule="evenodd" />

            {/* Subtitle: INVENTORY MANAGEMENT (Locked to Image 2 proportions) */}
            {showSubtitle && (
                <text
                    x={466}
                    y={472}
                    textAnchor="middle"
                    textLength={546}
                    lengthAdjust="spacing"
                    fill={variant === 'light-text' ? '#CBD5E1' : primaryInk}
                    fontSize={31}
                    fontWeight={700}
                    fontFamily='"Plus Jakarta Sans", "Inter", system-ui, sans-serif'
                >
                    {subtitle.text}
                </text>
            )}
        </svg>
    );
};

// src/components/layout/HeaderBar.tsx
import React, { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import {
    Building2,
    Bell,
    Layers,
    RefreshCw,
    MessageSquare,
    AlertTriangle,
    FileText,
    ChevronRight,
    Globe,
    CheckCheck,
} from 'lucide-react';
import type { ActiveTab, CurrencyCode, UserSession } from '../../types/vyte.ts';
import { CURRENCY_SYMBOLS } from '../../types/vyte.ts';

interface HeaderBarProps {
    activeTab: ActiveTab;
    inventorySubTab?: 'restock' | 'orders';
    userSession: UserSession | null;
    currency?: CurrencyCode;
    exchangeRate?: number;
    rateSourceLabel?: string;
    onRefreshRates?: () => void;
    totalSkus: number;
    pendingRestocks: number;
    pendingOrders: number;
    pendingQcBatches: number;
    totalInvoices: number;
    pendingInvoices: number;
    onNavigateFromNotification: (
        tab: ActiveTab,
        subTab?: 'restock' | 'orders'
    ) => void;
}

export const HeaderBar: React.FC<HeaderBarProps> = ({
                                                        activeTab,
                                                        inventorySubTab = 'restock',
                                                        userSession,
                                                        currency = 'MYR',
                                                        exchangeRate = 1.0,
                                                        rateSourceLabel = 'Live FX Rate Active',
                                                        onRefreshRates,
                                                        totalSkus,
                                                        pendingRestocks,
                                                        pendingOrders,
                                                        pendingQcBatches,
                                                        totalInvoices,
                                                        pendingInvoices,
                                                        onNavigateFromNotification,
                                                    }) => {
    const [isBellOpen, setIsBellOpen] = useState(false);
    const [acknowledgedCount, setAcknowledgedCount] = useState(0);
    const bellRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

    const currencySymbol = CURRENCY_SYMBOLS[currency] || 'RM';
    const convertedPreventableLoss = (762 * exchangeRate).toFixed(0);

    useEffect(() => {
        const handleClickOutside = (e: MouseEvent) => {
            if (bellRef.current && !bellRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) {
                setIsBellOpen(false);
            }
        };
        const handleEscape = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
            if (e.key === 'Escape') setIsBellOpen(false);
        };
        document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
        document.addEventListener('keydown', handleEscape);
        return () => {
            document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
            document.removeEventListener('keydown', handleEscape);
        };
    }, []);

    const titles: Record<ActiveTab, { title: string; subtitle: string }> = {
        mod1:
            inventorySubTab === 'orders'
                ? {
                    title: 'Order Status & NLP Customer Fulfillment',
                    subtitle: 'NLP customer order verification & live inventory reservation',
                }
                : {
                    title: 'Auto Restock & Universal Inventory',
                    subtitle: 'Threshold monitoring, spreadsheet import & supplier PO approval',
                },
        mod2: {
            title: 'Sales Analytics & AI Procurement Review',
            subtitle: 'Holding cost optimization & AI restock forecasting',
        },
        mod3: {
            title: 'Order Status & NLP Customer Fulfillment',
            subtitle: 'NLP customer order verification & live inventory reservation',
        },
        mod4: {
            title: 'Quality & Expiry Guardian (Orbyte HR Integrated)',
            subtitle: 'Batch expiry table, click-in AI SOPs & Orbyte HR staff task dispatch',
        },
        mod5: {
            title: 'Smart Invoices & AI Categorization',
            subtitle: 'Photo OCR / Excel upload, supplier bills & customer receivable verification',
        },
        logs: {
            title: 'History Log',
            subtitle: 'Centralized immutable audit trail across all modules and role permissions',
        },
    };

    const totalActionableAlerts =
        pendingRestocks + pendingOrders + pendingQcBatches + pendingInvoices;
    const unreadBadgeCount = Math.max(0, totalActionableAlerts - acknowledgedCount);

    const notificationItems = [
        {
            id: 'notif-skus',
            label: 'Active Universal SKUs',
            value: `${totalSkus} Items`,
            subtext: 'F&B, Retail & B2B Ready',
            subtextColor: 'text-emerald-600 font-semibold',
            valueColor: 'text-slate-900',
            iconBg: 'bg-blue-500',
            icon: Layers,
            onClick: () => onNavigateFromNotification('mod1', 'restock'),
        },
        {
            id: 'notif-restock',
            label: 'Auto-Restock Triggers',
            value: `${pendingRestocks} Pending`,
            subtext: 'Awaiting Owner Approval',
            subtextColor: 'text-amber-700 font-semibold',
            valueColor: 'text-orange-600',
            iconBg: 'bg-orange-500',
            icon: RefreshCw,
            onClick: () => onNavigateFromNotification('mod1', 'restock'),
        },
        {
            id: 'notif-orders',
            label: 'Customer Orders (NLP)',
            value: `${pendingOrders} Pending`,
            subtext: 'Open Order Sub-Tab',
            subtextColor: 'text-purple-600 font-semibold',
            valueColor: 'text-purple-700',
            iconBg: 'bg-purple-600',
            icon: MessageSquare,
            onClick: () => onNavigateFromNotification('mod1', 'orders'),
        },
        {
            // Point 8 Fixed: Quality Control font color corrected to rose-600 to match its rose icon and alert theme
            id: 'notif-qc',
            label: 'Quality / Expiry Risks',
            value: `${pendingQcBatches} Batches`,
            subtext: `${currencySymbol} ${convertedPreventableLoss} loss preventable â€¢ Assign HR`,
            subtextColor: 'text-rose-600 font-semibold',
            valueColor: 'text-rose-600',
            iconBg: 'bg-rose-500',
            icon: AlertTriangle,
            onClick: () => onNavigateFromNotification('mod4'),
        },
        {
            id: 'notif-invoices',
            label: 'AI Categorized Invoices',
            value: `${totalInvoices} Docs`,
            subtext: `${pendingInvoices} Pending Review`,
            subtextColor: 'text-emerald-700 font-semibold',
            valueColor: 'text-emerald-600',
            iconBg: 'bg-emerald-500',
            icon: FileText,
            onClick: () => onNavigateFromNotification('mod5'),
        },
    ];

    return (
        <header className="bg-white border border-slate-200/90 rounded-2xl px-6 py-3.5 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4 shrink-0 shadow-xs z-30">
            <div>
                <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">
                    {titles[activeTab].title}
                </h2>
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">{titles[activeTab].subtitle}</p>
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center gap-3.5">
                {/* Live Currency Exchange Rate Pill */}
                <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={onRefreshRates}
                    title={`${rateSourceLabel} (Click to refresh live FX rate)`}
                    className="hidden md:inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-emerald-50/90 hover:bg-emerald-100 border border-emerald-200 text-[11px] font-bold text-emerald-800 transition cursor-pointer"
                >
                    <Globe className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-600" />
                    <span>
            {currency} ({currencySymbol})
                        {currency !== 'MYR' ? ` â€¢ 1 RM = ${exchangeRate.toFixed(3)} ${currency}` : ' â€¢ Base Currency'}
          </span>
                </button>

                {/* User & Company Badge */}
                {userSession && (
                    <div className="text-right">
                        <p className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 leading-none">
                            {userSession.userName}{' '}
                            <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-emerald-600">
                ({userSession.userID})
              </span>
                        </p>
                        <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 flex items-center justify-end gap-1 mt-0.5">
                            <Building2 className="w-3 h-3" /> {userSession.companyName}
                        </p>
                    </div>
                )}

                {/* Notification Bell Dropdown */}
                <div ref={bellRef} className="relative pl-3 border-l border-slate-200">
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setIsBellOpen((prev) => !prev)}
                        title="Workspace Notifications & Live KPIs"
                        className={`relative p-2 rounded-xl border transition cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-center ${
                            isBellOpen
                                ? 'bg-emerald-50 border-[#2BB656] text-emerald-700 shadow-2xs'
                                : 'bg-slate-50 hover:bg-slate-100 border-slate-200 text-slate-700'
                        }`}
                    >
                        <Bell className="w-4 h-4" />
                        {unreadBadgeCount > 0 && (
                            <span className="absolute -top-1.5 -right-1.5 min-w-[16px] h-[16px] px-1 rounded-full bg-rose-500 text-white text-[9px] font-extrabold flex items-center justify-center shadow-2xs">
                {unreadBadgeCount}
              </span>
                        )}
                    </button>

                    {isBellOpen && (
                        <div className="absolute right-0 mt-2 w-72 bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xl overflow-hidden z-50 select-none">
                            <div className="px-3.5 py-2.5 bg-slate-900 text-white flex items-center justify-between">
                                <div>
                                    <h4 className="text-[11px] font-bold leading-tight">
                                        Live Hub Alerts &amp; KPIs
                                    </h4>
                                    <p className="text-[9px] text-slate-400">
                                        Click any item to jump to module
                                    </p>
                                </div>
                                <button
                                    type="button"
                                    onClick={() => setAcknowledgedCount(totalActionableAlerts)}
                                    className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-emerald-500/20 hover:bg-emerald-500/30 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-500/30 text-[9px] font-bold flex items-center gap-1 cursor-pointer transition"
                                >
                                    <CheckCheck className="w-3 h-3" /> Mark Read
                                </button>
                            </div>

                            <div className="p-2 space-y-1.5 max-h-80 overflow-y-auto">
                                {notificationItems.map((item) => {
                                    const Icon = item.icon;
                                    return (
                                        <button
                                            key={item.id}
                                            type="button"
                                            onClick={() => {
                                                item.onClick();
                                                setIsBellOpen(false);
                                            }}
                                            className="w-full text-left bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 active:scale-[0.99] px-2.5 py-2 rounded-lg border border-slate-200/80 hover:border-emerald-300 flex items-center justify-between gap-2 transition cursor-pointer group"
                                        >
                                            <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 min-w-0">
                                                <div
                                                    className={`w-7 h-7 rounded-lg ${item.iconBg} text-white flex items-center justify-center shrink-0`}
                                                >
                                                    <Icon className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                                                </div>
                                                <div className="min-w-0">
                                                    <div className="flex items-baseline gap-1.5">
                            <span
                                className={`text-xs font-extrabold leading-none ${item.valueColor}`}
                            >
                              {item.value}
                            </span>
                                                        <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-700 font-semibold truncate">
                              {item.label}
                            </span>
                                                    </div>
                                                    <span
                                                        className={`text-[10px] block truncate leading-tight mt-0.5 ${item.subtextColor}`}
                                                    >
                            {item.subtext}
                          </span>
                                                </div>
                                            </div>
                                            <ChevronRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400 group-hover:text-emerald-600 group-hover:translate-x-0.5 transition shrink-0" />
                                        </button>
                                    );
                                })}
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    )}
                </div>
            </div>
        </header>
    );
};

// src/components/layout/Sidebar.tsx
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import {
    Box,
    TrendingUp,
    AlertTriangle,
    FileText,
    Clock,
    Search,
    ChevronLeft,
    ChevronRight,
    Settings,
    LogOut,
    X,
    ShieldCheck,
    Volume2,
    FileCode,
    Sliders,
} from 'lucide-react';
import type { ActiveTab, CurrencyCode, UserRole, UserSession } from '../../types/vyte.ts';
import { VyteLogo } from '../common/VyteLogo';
import { VyteDropdown } from '../common/VyteDropdown';

interface SidebarProps {
    activeTab: ActiveTab;
    setActiveTab: (tab: ActiveTab) => void;
    role: UserRole;
    operatingCurrency: CurrencyCode;
    onCurrencyChange: (currency: CurrencyCode) => void;
    industryFilter?: string;
    setIndustryFilter?: (filter: string) => void;
    searchQuery: string;
    setSearchQuery: (q: string) => void;
    pendingRestockCount: number;
    pendingOrderCount: number;
    pendingQcCount: number;
    pendingInvoiceCount: number;
    auditLogCount: number;
    totalProductCount: number;
    userSession: UserSession | null;
    onLogout: () => void;
}

export const Sidebar: React.FC<SidebarProps> = ({
                                                    activeTab,
                                                    setActiveTab,
                                                    role,
                                                    operatingCurrency,
                                                    onCurrencyChange,
                                                    searchQuery,
                                                    setSearchQuery,
                                                    pendingRestockCount,
                                                    pendingOrderCount,
                                                    pendingQcCount,
                                                    pendingInvoiceCount,
                                                    auditLogCount,
                                                    userSession,
                                                    onLogout,
                                                }) => {
    const [isCollapsed, setIsCollapsed] = useState(false);
    const [showSettingsModal, setShowSettingsModal] = useState(false);

    const [tempCurrency, setTempCurrency] = useState<CurrencyCode>(operatingCurrency);
    const [defaultTurnoverTarget, setDefaultTurnoverTarget] = useState<'7' | '14' | '21' | '30' | '60'>('21');
    const [audioAlerts, setAudioAlerts] = useState(true);
    const [autoDraftPo, setAutoDraftPo] = useState(true);
    const [whatsappAutoAlerts, setWhatsappAutoAlerts] = useState(true);
    const [strictRbacLock, setStrictRbacLock] = useState(true);
    const [eInvoiceCompliance, setEInvoiceCompliance] = useState(true);

    useEffect(() => {
        setTempCurrency(operatingCurrency);
    }, [operatingCurrency, showSettingsModal]);

    const handleSaveSettings = () => {
        onCurrencyChange(tempCurrency);
        setShowSettingsModal(false);
    };

    const roleBadgeConfig: Record<UserRole, { label: string; style: string }> = {
        SME_OWNER: {
            label: 'Owner (Full Approval Access)',
            style: 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-800 border-emerald-200',
        },
        MANAGER: {
            label: 'Manager (Approval & Ops Access)',
            style: 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-800 border-blue-200',
        },
        OPERATIONS_STAFF: {
            label: 'Ops Staff (Read & Trigger Only)',
            style: 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-800 border-amber-200',
        },
    };

    const activeBadge = roleBadgeConfig[role];

    const coreModules = [
        {
            id: 'mod1' as ActiveTab,
            label: 'Restock & Orders',
            icon: Box,
            iconColor: 'text-emerald-600',
            badge: String(pendingRestockCount + pendingOrderCount),
            badgeClass: 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800',
        },
        {
            id: 'mod2' as ActiveTab,
            label: 'Sales Analytics',
            icon: TrendingUp,
            iconColor: 'text-blue-600',
            badge: 'AI',
            badgeClass: 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-700 rounded-md',
        },
        {
            id: 'mod4' as ActiveTab,
            label: 'Quality & Expiry',
            icon: AlertTriangle,
            iconColor: 'text-rose-600',
            badge: String(pendingQcCount),
            badgeClass: 'bg-rose-100 text-rose-700',
        },
        {
            id: 'mod5' as ActiveTab,
            label: 'Smart Invoices',
            icon: FileText,
            iconColor: 'text-emerald-600',
            badge: String(pendingInvoiceCount),
            badgeClass: 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700',
        },
        {
            id: 'logs' as ActiveTab,
            label: 'Audit Logs',
            icon: Clock,
            iconColor: 'text-slate-600',
            badge: String(auditLogCount),
            badgeClass: 'bg-slate-200 text-slate-800',
        },
    ];

    return (
        <>
            <aside
                className={`${
                    isCollapsed ? 'w-20' : 'w-64'
                } h-full bg-white border border-slate-200/90 rounded-2xl shadow-xs flex flex-col justify-between shrink-0 transition-all duration-300 ease-in-out select-none relative z-30`}
            >
                <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setIsCollapsed(!isCollapsed)}
                    title={isCollapsed ? 'Expand Sidebar' : 'Collapse Sidebar'}
                    className="w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-white border border-slate-200 shadow-sm flex items-center justify-center text-slate-500 hover:text-emerald-600 hover:border-emerald-300 absolute -right-3 top-8 z-40 transition cursor-pointer"
                >
                    {isCollapsed ? <ChevronRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> : <ChevronLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />}
                </button>

                <div className="flex flex-col flex-1 min-h-0 overflow-y-auto">
                    <div className="px-5 py-4 flex items-center justify-center border-b border-slate-100 shrink-0">
                        {isCollapsed ? (
                            <VyteLogo iconOnly={true} className="h-9 w-auto" />
                        ) : (
                            <VyteLogo variant="dark-text" showSubtitle={true} className="w-full max-w-[185px] h-auto" />
                        )}
                    </div>

                    <div className="p-3.5 shrink-0">
                        {isCollapsed ? (
                            <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() => setIsCollapsed(false)}
                                title="Search SKU or item"
                                className="w-full py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 hover:bg-slate-100 border border-slate-200/80 flex items-center justify-center text-slate-500 cursor-pointer"
                            >
                                <Search className="w-4 h-4" />
                            </button>
                        ) : (
                            <div className="relative">
                                <Search className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 absolute left-3 top-2.5" />
                                <input
                                    type="text"
                                    placeholder="Search SKU or item..."
                                    value={searchQuery}
                                    onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
                                    className="w-full pl-9 pr-3 py-2 bg-slate-50 text-slate-900 placeholder-slate-400 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-xs focus:outline-none focus:border-emerald-500 transition"
                                />
                            </div>
                        )}
                    </div>

                    <nav className="px-3 space-y-1.5">
                        {!isCollapsed && (
                            <p className="px-3 pt-1 pb-1 text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400">
                                CORE MODULES
                            </p>
                        )}

                        {coreModules.map((mod) => {
                            const Icon = mod.icon;
                            const isActive = activeTab === mod.id || (mod.id === 'mod1' && activeTab === 'mod3');
                            return (
                                <button
                                    key={mod.id}
                                    onClick={() => setActiveTab(mod.id)}
                                    title={isCollapsed ? mod.label : undefined}
                                    className={`w-full flex items-center ${
                                        isCollapsed
                                            ? 'justify-center px-2 py-3'
                                            : 'justify-between px-3.5 py-2.5'
                                    } rounded-xl text-sm transition-all cursor-pointer border ${
                                        isActive
                                            ? 'bg-[#EBFDF2] border-[#A7F3D0] text-emerald-950 font-bold shadow-[0_1px_2px_rgba(16,185,129,0.06)]'
                                            : 'border-transparent text-slate-700 font-medium hover:bg-slate-50'
                                    }`}
                                >
                  <span className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <Icon className={`w-4 h-4 shrink-0 ${mod.iconColor}`} />
                      {!isCollapsed && <span>{mod.label}</span>}
                  </span>

                                    {!isCollapsed && (
                                        <span className={`text-xs px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full font-bold ${mod.badgeClass}`}>
                      {mod.badge}
                    </span>
                                    )}
                                </button>
                            );
                        })}
                    </nav>
                </div>

                <div className="p-3 border-t border-slate-100 space-y-2 shrink-0">
                    <div
                        title={activeBadge.label}
                        className={`w-full rounded-xl border text-[11px] font-bold flex items-center ${
                            isCollapsed
                                ? 'justify-center py-2 px-1'
                                : 'justify-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2'
                        } ${activeBadge.style}`}

                                <Settings className="w-4 h-4" />
                            </button>
                            <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={onLogout}
                                title="Log Out"
                                className="w-full py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-rose-50/70 hover:bg-rose-100 text-rose-600 border border-rose-200/70 flex items-center justify-center transition cursor-pointer"
                            >
                                <LogOut className="w-4 h-4" />
                            </button>
                        </div>
                    ) : (
                        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                            <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() => setShowSettingsModal(true)}
                                className="py-2 px-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 hover:bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 hover:text-slate-900 border border-slate-200/90 text-xs font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition cursor-pointer"

                                    <VyteDropdown<CurrencyCode>

// src/components/LoadingScreen.tsx
import React, { useEffect, useState, useRef } from 'react';
import startAnimData from './start_anim.json';

export interface LoadingScreenProps {
    isOpen?: boolean;
    message?: string;
    onClose?: () => void;
}

interface StreamParticle {
    id: number;
    x: number;
    y: number;
    length: number;
    size: number;
    speed: number;
    color: string;
    opacity: number;
    type: 'streak' | 'dot' | 'ground';
}

const INITIAL_PARTICLES: StreamParticle[] = [
    { id: 1, x: 820, y: 135, length: 65, size: 4, speed: 9.5, color: '#2BB656', opacity: 0.45, type: 'streak' },

export const LoadingScreen: React.FC<LoadingScreenProps> = ({
                                                                isOpen = true,
                                                                message = 'Syncing Vyte Inventory Engine...',
                                                                onClose,
                                                            }) => {
    const [elapsedMs, setElapsedMs] = useState<number>(0);
    const rafRef = useRef<number | null>(null);
    const startTimeRef = useRef<number | null>(null);

    const { colors } = startAnimData.designTokens;
    const { glyphs } = startAnimData.logoGeometry;

    useEffect(() => {
        if (!isOpen) {
            setElapsedMs(0);
            startTimeRef.current = null;
            return;
        }

        const tick = (timestamp: number) => {
            if (startTimeRef.current === null) startTimeRef.current = timestamp;
            setElapsedMs(timestamp - startTimeRef.current);
            rafRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
        };

        rafRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
        return () => {
            if (rafRef.current) cancelAnimationFrame(rafRef.current);
        };
    }, [isOpen]);

    if (!isOpen) return null;

    const sproutProgress = Math.min(1, elapsedMs / 420);
    const sproutEase = 1 - Math.pow(1 - sproutProgress, 3);

    const walkTime = Math.max(0, elapsedMs - 250) / 1000;
    const walkPhase = walkTime * 9.2;

    const vBobY = sproutEase * Math.abs(Math.sin(walkPhase)) * -6;
    const vLeanDeg = sproutEase * (4.5 + Math.sin(walkPhase * 2) * 1.2);
    const cartBounceY = sproutEase * Math.sin(walkPhase * 2 - 0.7) * 2.2;
    const wheelRotation = (walkTime * 340) % 360;

    const hipX = 238;
    const hipY = 342 + vBobY;

    const computeLegJoints = (phaseOffset: number) => {
        const phase = walkPhase + phaseOffset;
        const thighLen = 56 * sproutEase;
        const shinLen = 54 * sproutEase;
        const footLen = 20 * sproutEase;

        const thighAngle = Math.sin(phase) * 0.52 + 0.08;
        const kneeBend = Math.max(0, -Math.cos(phase + 0.35)) * 0.85;
        const shinAngle = thighAngle - kneeBend;

        const kneeX = hipX + Math.sin(thighAngle) * thighLen;
        const kneeY = hipY + Math.cos(thighAngle) * thighLen;

        const ankleX = kneeX + Math.sin(shinAngle) * shinLen;
        const ankleY = kneeY + Math.cos(shinAngle) * shinLen;

        const footAngle = shinAngle + 1.45;
        const toeX = ankleX + Math.sin(footAngle) * footLen;
        const toeY = ankleY + Math.cos(footAngle) * footLen;

        return { kneeX, kneeY, ankleX, ankleY, toeX, toeY };
    };

    const backLeg = computeLegJoints(Math.PI);
    const frontLeg = computeLegJoints(0);

    const shoulderX = 296;
    const shoulderY = 248 + vBobY;
    const handleGripX = 334;
    const handleGripY = 262 + cartBounceY;
    const elbowX = (shoulderX + handleGripX) / 2 - 6 * sproutEase;
    const elbowY = (shoulderY + handleGripY) / 2 + 18 * sproutEase;

    return (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[9998] bg-slate-900/45 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4 select-none">
            {/* Compact Pop-Up Modal Box */}
            <div className="relative bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-3xl shadow-2xl w-full max-w-md px-6 py-6 flex flex-col items-center justify-center overflow-hidden">
                {/* Subtle Green Radial Accent Inside Pop-Up */}
                <div
                    style={{
                        position: 'absolute',
                        width: '280px',
                        height: '280px',
                        borderRadius: '50%',
                        background: `radial-gradient(circle, ${colors.vyteGreenGlow} 0%, rgba(255,255,255,0) 70%)`,
                        pointerEvents: 'none',
                    }}
                />

                <svg
                    viewBox="88.5 73 800 440"
                    preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet"
                    className="w-full max-w-[320px] h-auto block mx-auto overflow-visible relative z-10"
                    aria-label="Vyte Loading Animation"
                >
                    <defs>
                        <filter id="vyte-cart-shadow" x="-20%" y="-20%" width="140%" height="140%">
                            <feDropShadow dx="0" dy="10" stdDeviation="8" floodColor="#0B0C0E" floodOpacity="0.10" />
                        </filter>
                    </defs>

                    <g style={{ pointerEvents: 'none' }}>
                        <line
                            x1={115}
                            y1={466}
                            x2={862}
                            y2={466}
                            stroke="#E2E8F0"
                            strokeWidth={3}
                            strokeLinecap="round"
                        />

                        {INITIAL_PARTICLES.map((p) => {
                            const span = 740;
                            const rawX = (((p.x - (elapsedMs * p.speed) / 16) % span) + span) % span + 115;

                            if (p.type === 'dot') {
                                return (
                                    <circle
                                        key={p.id}
                                        cx={rawX}
                                        cy={p.y + Math.sin(elapsedMs / 240 + p.id) * 4}
                                        r={p.size}
                                        fill={p.color}
                                        opacity={p.opacity * sproutEase}
                                    />
                                );
                            }

                            return (
                                <line
                                    key={p.id}
                                    x1={rawX}
                                    y1={p.y}
                                    x2={rawX + p.length}
                                    y2={p.y}
                                    stroke={p.color}
                                    strokeWidth={p.size}
                                    strokeLinecap="round"
                                    opacity={p.opacity * sproutEase}
                                />
                            );
                        })}
                    </g>

                    {sproutEase > 0.02 && (
                        <g
                            stroke="#334155"
                            strokeWidth={10}
                            strokeLinecap="round"
                            strokeLinejoin="round"
                            fill="none"
                        >
                            <path
                                d={`M ${hipX - 6} ${hipY} L ${backLeg.kneeX} ${backLeg.kneeY} L ${backLeg.ankleX} ${backLeg.ankleY} L ${backLeg.toeX} ${backLeg.toeY}`}
                            />
                            <path
                                d={`M ${shoulderX - 8} ${shoulderY - 10} L ${elbowX - 4} ${elbowY - 10} L ${handleGripX} ${handleGripY - 12}`}
                                strokeWidth={8}
                            />
                        </g>
                    )}

                    <g
                        filter="url(#vyte-cart-shadow)"
                        style={{
                            transform: `translate(22px, ${cartBounceY}px)`,
                        }}
                    >
                        <path
                            d="M 342 418 L 342 252 Q 342 238 326 238 L 312 238"
                            fill="none"
                            stroke={colors.inkBlack}
                            strokeWidth={11}
                            strokeLinecap="round"
                            strokeLinejoin="round"
                            opacity={sproutEase}
                        />
                        <circle
                            cx={312}
                            cy={238}
                            r={8}
                            fill={colors.vyteGreen}
                            opacity={sproutEase}
                        />

                        <g opacity={sproutEase}>
                            <rect
                                x={330}
                                y={414}
                                width={490}
                                height={20}
                                rx={10}
                                fill={colors.inkBlack}
                            />
                            <rect
                                x={344}
                                y={420}
                                width={462}
                                height={6}
                                rx={3}
                                fill={colors.vyteGreen}
                            />
                            <rect
                                x={804}
                                y={392}
                                width={12}
                                height={30}
                                rx={5}
                                fill={colors.inkBlack}
                            />
                        </g>

                        <g style={{ transform: 'translate(14px, -6px)' }}>
                            <path d={glyphs.y_leftFork.d} fill={glyphs.y_leftFork.fill} />

========== FILE: C:\Users\Jayden\WebstormProjects\Vyte\orbyteflow\src\components\SplashScreen.tsx ==========

// src/components/SplashScreen.tsx
import React, { useEffect, useState, useRef } from 'react';
import startAnimData from './start_anim.json';

export interface SplashScreenProps {
    onComplete?: () => void;
    animationData?: typeof startAnimData;
    autoDismiss?: boolean;
}

function springPhysics(t: number, damping = 5.5, frequency = 11.5): number {
    if (t <= 0) return 0;
    if (t >= 1) return 1;
    const decay = Math.exp(-damping * t);
    return 1 - decay * (Math.cos(frequency * t) + (damping / frequency) * Math.sin(frequency * t));
}

function progressBetween(elapsed: number, startMs: number, durationMs: number): number {
    return Math.min(1, Math.max(0, (elapsed - startMs) / durationMs));
}

interface MiniParcel {
    id: string;
    shelf: number;
    x: number;
    y: number;
    w: number;
    h: number;
    tone: 'kraft' | 'dark' | 'green';
    delayMs: number;
}

export const SplashScreen: React.FC<SplashScreenProps> = ({
                                                              onComplete,
                                                              animationData = startAnimData,
                                                              autoDismiss = true,
                                                          }) => {
    const [elapsedMs, setElapsedMs] = useState<number>(0);
    const [isPlaying, setIsPlaying] = useState<boolean>(true);
    const [isExiting, setIsExiting] = useState<boolean>(false);
    const rafRef = useRef<number | null>(null);
    const lastTimeRef = useRef<number | null>(null);

    const totalDuration = 3400;
    const { colors } = animationData.designTokens;
    const { glyphs, subtitle } = animationData.logoGeometry;

    const tMorph = springPhysics(progressBetween(elapsedMs, 500, 600), 6.2, 10.5);
    const tPackCompress = springPhysics(progressBetween(elapsedMs, 1350, 500), 5.8, 11.0);
    const tCartonRise = springPhysics(progressBetween(elapsedMs, 1400, 540), 5.2, 12.0);
    const tFlapsClose = springPhysics(progressBetween(elapsedMs, 1650, 400), 5.0, 13.0);
    const tTapeSeal = springPhysics(progressBetween(elapsedMs, 1850, 300), 6.5, 11.0);

    const shipRaw = progressBetween(elapsedMs, 2100, 520);
    const shipAnticipation = shipRaw > 0 && shipRaw < 0.22 ? -Math.sin((shipRaw / 0.22) * Math.PI) * 28 : 0;
    const shipLaunch = shipRaw >= 0.18 ? Math.pow((shipRaw - 0.18) / 0.82, 2.3) * 1180 : 0;
    const shipTranslateX = shipAnticipation + shipLaunch;
    const shipScaleX = shipRaw > 0.2 ? 1 + Math.min(0.18, (shipRaw - 0.2) * 0.35) : 1 - Math.max(0, shipRaw * 0.25);
    const shipScaleY = shipRaw > 0.2 ? 1 - Math.min(0.12, (shipRaw - 0.2) * 0.22) : 1 + Math.max(0, shipRaw * 0.2);

    const miniParcels: MiniParcel[] = [
        { id: 'p0', shelf: 0, x: 314, y: 120, w: 50, h: 32, tone: 'kraft', delayMs: 650 },
        { id: 'p1', shelf: 0, x: 372, y: 116, w: 56, h: 36, tone: 'green', delayMs: 710 },
        { id: 'p2', shelf: 0, x: 436, y: 122, w: 48, h: 30, tone: 'dark', delayMs: 770 },
        { id: 'p3', shelf: 1, x: 316, y: 192, w: 58, h: 34, tone: 'dark', delayMs: 730 },
        { id: 'p4', shelf: 1, x: 382, y: 190, w: 48, h: 36, tone: 'kraft', delayMs: 790 },
        { id: 'p5', shelf: 1, x: 438, y: 194, w: 46, h: 32, tone: 'green', delayMs: 850 },
        { id: 'p6', shelf: 2, x: 314, y: 264, w: 52, h: 34, tone: 'green', delayMs: 810 },
        { id: 'p7', shelf: 2, x: 374, y: 262, w: 60, h: 36, tone: 'kraft', delayMs: 870 },
        { id: 'p8', shelf: 2, x: 442, y: 266, w: 44, h: 32, tone: 'dark', delayMs: 930 },
    ];

    useEffect(() => {
        if (!isPlaying) {
            lastTimeRef.current = null;
            return;
        }

        const tick = (timestamp: number) => {
            if (lastTimeRef.current === null) lastTimeRef.current = timestamp;
            const delta = Math.min(32, timestamp - lastTimeRef.current);
            lastTimeRef.current = timestamp;

            setElapsedMs((prev) => {
                const next = prev + delta;
                if (next >= totalDuration) {
                    setIsPlaying(false);
                    if (autoDismiss) {
                        setTimeout(() => setIsExiting(true), 150);
                        setTimeout(() => onComplete?.(), 580);
                    }
                    return totalDuration;
                }
                return next;
            });

            rafRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
        };

        rafRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
        return () => {
            if (rafRef.current) cancelAnimationFrame(rafRef.current);
        };
    }, [isPlaying, totalDuration, autoDismiss, onComplete]);

    const getGlyphSpring = (delayOffsetMs: number) => {
        const raw = progressBetween(elapsedMs, 2240 + delayOffsetMs, 760);
        const s = springPhysics(raw, 5.2, 11.5);
        return {
            translateX: -420 * (1 - s),
            translateY: 24 * (1 - s),
            scale: 0.82 + 0.18 * s,
            opacity: Math.min(1, raw * 3.8),
        };
    };

    const vSpring = getGlyphSpring(0);
    const ySpring = getGlyphSpring(70);
    const tSpring = getGlyphSpring(140);
    const eSpring = getGlyphSpring(210);
    const subSpring = getGlyphSpring(310);

    return (
        <div
            style={{
                position: 'fixed',
                top: 0,
                left: 0,
                width: '100vw',
                height: '100dvh',
                zIndex: 9999,
                display: 'grid',
                placeItems: 'center',
                backgroundColor: colors.surfaceOffWhite,
                opacity: isExiting ? 0 : 1,
                pointerEvents: isExiting ? 'none' : 'auto',
                transition: 'opacity 420ms cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1)',
                fontFamily: '"Plus Jakarta Sans", "Inter", system-ui, sans-serif',
                userSelect: 'none',
                overflow: 'hidden',
            }}
        >
            {/* Ambient Radial Glow (Centered dynamically in viewport) */}
            <div
                style={{
                    position: 'absolute',
                    width: 'min(80vw, 640px)',
                    height: 'min(80vw, 640px)',
                    borderRadius: '50%',
                    background: `radial-gradient(circle, ${colors.vyteGreenGlow} 0%, rgba(246,247,244,0) 70%)`,
                    transform: `scale(${0.85 + 0.35 * tCartonRise})`,
                    opacity: elapsedMs > 2200 ? 0.95 : 0.55,
                    transition: 'opacity 400ms ease',
                    pointerEvents: 'none',
                }}
            />

            <svg
                viewBox={animationData.logoGeometry.viewBox}
                preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet"
                style={{
                    width: 'min(88vw, 740px)',
                    maxHeight: '82dvh',
                    height: 'auto',
                    display: 'block',
                    margin: '0 auto',
                    overflow: 'visible',
                }}
            >
                <defs>
                    <filter id="vyte-elevated-shadow" x="-25%" y="-25%" width="150%" height="150%">
                        <feDropShadow dx="0" dy="16" stdDeviation="16" floodColor="#0B0C0E" floodOpacity="0.11" />
                    </filter>
                    <linearGradient id="kraft-box-grad" x1="0%" y1="0%" x2="100%" y2="100%">
                        <stop offset="0%" stopColor="#DFA366" />
                        <stop offset="100%" stopColor="#C48345" />
                    </linearGradient>
                    <linearGradient id="kraft-flap-grad" x1="0%" y1="0%" x2="0%" y2="100%">
                        <stop offset="0%" stopColor="#ECCFA9" />
                        <stop offset="100%" stopColor="#D49658" />
                    </linearGradient>
                </defs>

                {/* ACT 1 - 3: DATABASE -> LOGISTICS DRAWER -> BOXED UP & SHIPPED */}
                {shipRaw < 0.98 && (
                    <g
                        style={{
                            transformOrigin: '400px 235px',
                            transform: `translate(${shipTranslateX}px, 0px) scale(${shipScaleX}, ${shipScaleY})`,
                            opacity: shipRaw > 0.82 ? Math.max(0, 1 - (shipRaw - 0.82) * 5.5) : 1,
                        }}
                    >
                        {/* 1. DATABASE / LOGISTICS DRAWER UNIT */}
                        <g
                            filter="url(#vyte-elevated-shadow)"
                            style={{
                                transformOrigin: '400px 250px',
                                transform: `translateY(${tPackCompress * 34}px) scale(${1 - tPackCompress * 0.22})`,
                                opacity: 1 - Math.max(0, (tFlapsClose - 0.45) * 1.9),
                            }}
                        >
                            <g
                                style={{
                                    transformOrigin: '400px 215px',
                                    transform: `scale(${0.84 + 0.16 * tMorph})`,
                                    opacity: Math.min(1, tMorph * 1.4),
                                }}
                            >
                                <rect x={274} y={84} width={252} height={260} rx={24} fill={colors.inkBlack} />
                                <rect x={284} y={94} width={232} height={240} rx={18} fill="#F8FAF7" />
                                <rect x={294} y={104} width={6} height={220} rx={3} fill="#E2E5DF" />
                                <rect x={500} y={104} width={6} height={220} rx={3} fill="#E2E5DF" />
                            </g>

                            {[0, 1, 2].map((tierIdx) => {
                                const tierY = 104 + tierIdx * 72;
                                const dbEnter = springPhysics(progressBetween(elapsedMs, tierIdx * 90, 500), 4.8, 12.0);
                                const dbScale = 0.7 + 0.3 * dbEnter;
                                const dbTranslateY = (1 - dbEnter) * -45;

                                return (
                                    <g
                                        key={`db-tier-${tierIdx}`}
                                        style={{
                                            transformOrigin: `400px ${tierY + 28}px`,
                                            transform: `translateY(${dbTranslateY}px) scale(${dbScale})`,
                                            opacity: Math.min(1, dbEnter * 2.5),
                                        }}
                                    >
                                        <rect
                                            x={300 - (1 - tMorph) * 10}
                                            y={tierY}
                                            width={200 + (1 - tMorph) * 20}
                                            height={56}
                                            rx={tMorph > 0.5 ? 12 : 26}
                                            fill={tMorph > 0.5 ? '#E7ECE4' : colors.inkBlack}
                                        />

                                        <ellipse
                                            cx={400}
                                            cy={tierY + 13}
                                            rx={106}
                                            ry={12}
                                            fill="#1F242D"
                                            style={{ opacity: Math.max(0, 1 - tMorph * 1.35) }}
                                        />

                                        <g style={{ opacity: Math.max(0, 1 - tMorph * 1.5) }}>
                                            <circle cx={324} cy={tierY + 34} r={6} fill={colors.vyteGreen} />
                                            <rect x={340} y={tierY + 31} width={75} height={6} rx={3} fill="#2E3540" />
                                            <rect x={425} y={tierY + 31} width={42} height={6} rx={3} fill={colors.vyteGreen} opacity={0.65} />
                                        </g>

                                        {miniParcels
                                            .filter((p) => p.shelf === tierIdx)
                                            .map((parcel) => {
                                                const pRaw = progressBetween(elapsedMs, parcel.delayMs, 450);
                                                const pSpring = springPhysics(pRaw, 4.4, 13.5);
                                                const fillMap = {
                                                    kraft: '#D49658',
                                                    dark: colors.inkBlack,
                                                    green: colors.vyteGreen,
                                                };
                                                return (
                                                    <g
                                                        key={parcel.id}
                                                        style={{
                                                            transformOrigin: `${parcel.x + parcel.w / 2}px ${parcel.y + parcel.h}px`,
                                                            transform: `translateY(${(1 - pSpring) * -26}px) scale(${0.3 + 0.7 * pSpring})`,
                                                            opacity: Math.min(1, pRaw * 4),
                                                        }}
                                                    >
                                                        <rect
                                                            x={parcel.x}
                                                            y={parcel.y}
                                                            width={parcel.w}
                                                            height={parcel.h}
                                                            rx={6}
                                                            fill={fillMap[parcel.tone]}
                                                        />
                                                        <rect
                                                            x={parcel.x + parcel.w / 2 - 4}
                                                            y={parcel.y}
                                                            width={8}
                                                            height={parcel.h * 0.45}
                                                            rx={1.5}
                                                            fill={parcel.tone === 'green' ? '#FFFFFF' : colors.vyteGreen}
                                                            opacity={0.9}
                                                        />
                                                        <rect
                                                            x={parcel.x + 6}
                                                            y={parcel.y + parcel.h - 11}
                                                            width={16}
                                                            height={6}
                                                            rx={1.5}
                                                            fill="#FFFFFF"
                                                            opacity={0.85}
                                                        />
                                                    </g>
                                                );
                                            })}

                                        <g style={{ opacity: Math.min(1, tMorph * 1.5) }}>
                                            <rect x={300} y={tierY + 38} width={200} height={20} rx={8} fill="#CFD6C9" />
                                            <rect
                                                x={380}
                                                y={tierY + 45}
                                                width={40}
                                                height={6}
                                                rx={3}
                                                fill={tierIdx === 1 ? colors.vyteGreen : colors.inkBlack}
                                            />
                                        </g>
                                    </g>
                                );
                            })}
                        </g>

                        {/* 2. MASTER CORRUGATED LOGISTICS CARTON */}
                        {elapsedMs >= 1350 && (
                            <g
                                filter="url(#vyte-elevated-shadow)"
                                style={{
                                    transformOrigin: '400px 270px',
                                    transform: `translateY(${(1 - tCartonRise) * 140}px) scale(${0.86 + 0.14 * tCartonRise})`,
                                    opacity: Math.min(1, progressBetween(elapsedMs, 1350, 200) * 2),
                                }}
                            >
                                <g
                                    style={{
                                        transformOrigin: '400px 168px',
                                        transform: `scaleY(${1 - tFlapsClose * 0.88})`,
                                        opacity: 1 - tFlapsClose * 0.6,
                                    }}
                                >
                                    <polygon points="264,168 310,124 398,124 398,168" fill="url(#kraft-flap-grad)" />
                                    <polygon points="536,168 490,124 402,124 402,168" fill="url(#kraft-flap-grad)" />
                                </g>

                                <rect x={264} y={166} width={272} height={186} rx={20} fill="url(#kraft-box-grad)" />

                                <path
                                    d="M 512 166 L 536 166 L 536 332 C 536 343 527 352 516 352 L 512 352 Z"
                                    fill="#A96B31"
                                />

                                <line
                                    x1={276}
                                    y1={174}
                                    x2={524}
                                    y2={174}
                                    stroke="#9D622B"
                                    strokeWidth={3}
                                    strokeLinecap="round"
                                    opacity={tFlapsClose}
                                />

                                <rect
                                    x={384}
                                    y={160}
                                    width={32}
                                    height={196}
                                    rx={6}
                                    fill={colors.vyteGreen}
                                    style={{
                                        transformOrigin: '400px 160px',
                                        transform: `scaleY(${tTapeSeal})`,
                                        opacity: tTapeSeal > 0.02 ? 1 : 0,
                                    }}
                                />

                                <g
                                    transform="translate(290, 250)"
                                    style={{
                                        transformOrigin: '44px 30px',
                                        transform: `scale(${0.6 + 0.4 * tTapeSeal})`,
                                        opacity: Math.min(1, tTapeSeal * 1.8),
                                    }}
                                >
                                    <rect x={0} y={0} width={84} height={58} rx={8} fill="#FFFFFF" />
                                    <circle cx={16} cy={16} r={5} fill={colors.vyteGreen} />
                                    <rect x={26} y={12} width={44} height={8} rx={3} fill={colors.inkBlack} />
                                    <rect x={11} y={28} width={62} height={5} rx={2.5} fill="#94A3B8" />
                                    <rect x={11} y={38} width={46} height={5} rx={2.5} fill="#CBD5E1" />
                                    <rect x={11} y={47} width={56} height={4} rx={2} fill={colors.vyteGreen} />
                                </g>

                                {shipRaw > 0.18 && (
                                    <g style={{ opacity: Math.sin(((shipRaw - 0.18) / 0.82) * Math.PI) }}>
                                        <rect x={175} y={195} width={68} height={7} rx={3.5} fill={colors.vyteGreen} />
                                        <rect x={135} y={248} width={108} height={8} rx={4} fill={colors.inkBlack} />
                                        <rect x={190} y={302} width={52} height={7} rx={3.5} fill={colors.vyteGreen} />
                                    </g>
                                )}
                            </g>
                        )}
                    </g>
                )}

                {/* ACT 4: VYTE LOGO STAGGERED SPRING REVEAL (Shifted -65.5px X & -20px Y so midpoint 465.5 aligns with viewBox center 400) */}
                {elapsedMs >= 2220 && (
                    <g transform="translate(-65.5, -20)" style={{ pointerEvents: 'none' }}>
                        <g
                            style={{
                                transformOrigin: '245px 235px',
                                transform: `translate(${vSpring.translateX}px, ${vSpring.translateY}px) scale(${vSpring.scale})`,
                                opacity: vSpring.opacity,
                            }}
                        >
                            <path d={glyphs.V_leftLeg.d} fill={glyphs.V_leftLeg.fill} />
                            <path d={glyphs.V_rightGreenSlash.d} fill={glyphs.V_rightGreenSlash.fill} />
                        </g>

                        <g
                            style={{
                                transformOrigin: '410px 295px',
                                transform: `translate(${ySpring.translateX}px, ${ySpring.translateY}px) scale(${ySpring.scale})`,
                                opacity: ySpring.opacity,
                            }}
                        >
                            <path d={glyphs.y_leftFork.d} fill={glyphs.y_leftFork.fill} />
                            <path d={glyphs.y_descender.d} fill={glyphs.y_descender.fill} />
                            <circle
                                cx={glyphs.y_topGreenDot.cx}
                                cy={glyphs.y_topGreenDot.cy}
                                r={glyphs.y_topGreenDot.r}
                                fill={glyphs.y_topGreenDot.fill}
                            />
                            <circle
                                cx={glyphs.y_bottomGreenDot.cx}
                                cy={glyphs.y_bottomGreenDot.cy}
                                r={glyphs.y_bottomGreenDot.r}
                                fill={glyphs.y_bottomGreenDot.fill}
                            />
                        </g>

                        <g
                            style={{
                                transformOrigin: '560px 245px',
                                transform: `translate(${tSpring.translateX}px, ${tSpring.translateY}px) scale(${tSpring.scale})`,
                                opacity: tSpring.opacity,
                            }}
                        >
                            <path d={glyphs.t_body.d} fill={glyphs.t_body.fill} />
                        </g>

                        <g
                            style={{
                                transformOrigin: '715px 270px',
                                transform: `translate(${eSpring.translateX}px, ${eSpring.translateY}px) scale(${eSpring.scale})`,
                                opacity: eSpring.opacity,
                            }}
                        >
                            <path d={glyphs.e_body.d} fill={glyphs.e_body.fill} fillRule="evenodd" />
                        </g>

                        <text
                            x={subtitle.x}
                            y={subtitle.y}
                            textAnchor="middle"
                            fill={subtitle.fill}
                            style={{
                                fontSize: `${subtitle.fontSize}px`,
                                fontWeight: subtitle.fontWeight,
                                letterSpacing: subtitle.letterSpacing,
                                opacity: subSpring.opacity,
                                transform: `translateY(${(1 - subSpring.opacity) * 14}px)`,
                            }}
                        >
                            {subtitle.text}
                        </text>
                    </g>
                )}
            </svg>
        </div>
    );
};

export default SplashScreen;
