========== FILE: C:\Users\Jayden\WebstormProjects\Vyte\orbyteflow\src\modules\module1-restock\AddProductModal.tsx ==========

// src/modules/module1-restock/AddProductModal.tsx
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import type { CurrencyCode, IndustryProfile, NewProductForm } from '../../types/vyte.ts';
import { CURRENCY_SYMBOLS } from '../../types/vyte.ts';
import { VyteDropdown } from '../../components/common/VyteDropdown';

interface AddProductModalProps {
    isOpen: boolean;
    currency?: CurrencyCode;
    onClose: () => void;
    onSaveProduct: (form: NewProductForm) => void;
}

interface FormDraft {
    name: string;
    sku: string;
    category: string;
    industry: IndustryProfile;
    uom: string;
    stock: string;
    minThreshold: string;
    reorderQty: string;
    priceRM: string;
    unitCostRM: string;
    supplierName: string;
    supplierContact: string;
    batchNo: string;
    expiryDate: string;
    storageCondition: 'Ambient' | 'Chilled' | 'Frozen';
    variantSpec: string;
    b2bMoq: string;
    serialOrWarranty: string;
}

type ValidationErrors = Partial<Record<keyof FormDraft, string>>;
const ADD_PROD_DRAFT_KEY = 'vyte_add_product_draft_v2';

const isValidWhatsAppNumber = (phone: string): boolean => {
    const trimmed = phone.trim();
    const myPhoneRegex = /^(\+?60|0)1[0-9]-?[0-9]{7,8}$/;
    return myPhoneRegex.test(trimmed);
};

export const AddProductModal: React.FC<AddProductModalProps> = ({
                                                                    isOpen,
                                                                    currency = 'MYR',
                                                                    onClose,
                                                                    onSaveProduct,
                                                                }) => {
    const currencySymbol = CURRENCY_SYMBOLS[currency] || 'RM';

    const [draft, setDraft] = useState<FormDraft>(() => {
        try {
            const saved = localStorage.getItem(ADD_PROD_DRAFT_KEY);
            if (saved) return JSON.parse(saved) as FormDraft;
        } catch {
            // ignore
        }
        return {
            name: '',
            sku: '',
            category: 'F&B Raw Ingredient',
            industry: 'FNB_PERISHABLE',
            uom: 'kg',
            stock: '',
            minThreshold: '',
            reorderQty: '',
            priceRM: '',
            unitCostRM: '',
            supplierName: '',
            supplierContact: '',
            batchNo: '',
            expiryDate: '',
            storageCondition: 'Chilled',
            variantSpec: '',
            b2bMoq: '',
            serialOrWarranty: '',
        };
    });

    React.useEffect(() => {
        localStorage.setItem(ADD_PROD_DRAFT_KEY, JSON.stringify(draft));
    }, [draft]);

    const [errors, setErrors] = useState<ValidationErrors>({});

    if (!isOpen) return null;

    const validateField = (field: keyof FormDraft, value: string, currentDraft: FormDraft): string => {
        switch (field) {
            case 'name':
                if (!value.trim()) return 'Product name is required.';
                if (value.trim().length < 3) return 'Product name must be at least 3 characters.';
                return '';

            case 'sku':
                if (value.trim() && !/^[A-Za-z0-9-_]{3,20}$/.test(value.trim())) {
                    return 'SKU must be 3â€“20 alphanumeric characters (e.g., FNB-4886).';
                }
                return '';

            case 'priceRM': {
                const trimmed = value.trim();
                if (!trimmed) return 'Selling price is required.';
                if (trimmed.startsWith('0')) return 'Selling price cannot start with 0.';
                if (!/^[1-9]\d*(\.\d{1,2})?$/.test(trimmed)) {
                    return 'Enter a valid price greater than 0 (e.g., 45.00).';
                }
                const num = Number(trimmed);
                if (num > 1000000) return `Selling price cannot exceed ${currencySymbol} 1,000,000.`;
                return '';
            }

            case 'stock': {
                const trimmed = value.trim();
                if (!trimmed) return 'Initial current stock is required.';
                if (!/^\d+$/.test(trimmed)) return 'Initial stock must be whole numbers (0-9).';
                const num = Number(trimmed);
                if (num > 999999) return 'Initial stock cannot exceed 999,999.';
                return '';
            }

            case 'minThreshold': {
                const trimmed = value.trim();
                if (!trimmed) return 'Alert threshold (min stock) is required.';
                if (trimmed.startsWith('0') || !/^[1-9]\d*$/.test(trimmed)) {
                    return 'Alert threshold cannot start with 0 and must be at least 1.';
                }
                if (Number(trimmed) > 99999) return 'Alert threshold cannot exceed 99,999.';
                return '';
            }

            case 'reorderQty': {
                const trimmed = value.trim();
                if (!trimmed) return 'Default reorder quantity is required.';
                if (trimmed.startsWith('0') || !/^[1-9]\d*$/.test(trimmed)) {
                    return 'Reorder quantity cannot start with 0 and must be at least 1.';
                }
                return '';
            }

            case 'supplierName':
                if (!value.trim()) return 'Supplier business name is required.';
                if (value.trim().length < 2) return 'Supplier name must be at least 2 characters.';
                return '';

            case 'supplierContact':
                if (!value.trim()) return 'Supplier WhatsApp number is required.';
                if (!isValidWhatsAppNumber(value)) {
                    return 'Invalid WhatsApp number. Use MY format (e.g., +6012-3456789 or 0123456789).';
                }
                return '';

            case 'batchNo':
                if (currentDraft.industry === 'FNB_PERISHABLE' && !value.trim()) {
                    return 'Batch number is required for F&B items.';
                }
                return '';

            case 'expiryDate':
                if (currentDraft.industry === 'FNB_PERISHABLE' && !value.trim()) {
                    return 'Expiry date is required for F&B items.';
                }
                return '';

            case 'variantSpec':
                if (currentDraft.industry === 'RETAIL_VARIANT' && !value.trim()) {
                    return 'Variant specification is required.';
                }
                return '';

            case 'b2bMoq':
                if (currentDraft.industry === 'B2B_RAW_MATERIAL' && !value.trim()) {
                    return 'B2B MOQ and lead time constraint is required.';
                }
                return '';

            default:
                return '';
        }
    };

    const handleChange = (field: keyof FormDraft, rawValue: string) => {
        const updated = { ...draft, [field]: rawValue };
        setDraft(updated);

        const fieldError = validateField(field, rawValue, updated);
        setErrors((prev) => ({ ...prev, [field]: fieldError }));
    };

    const validateAll = (): boolean => {
        const fieldsToCheck: (keyof FormDraft)[] = [
            'name',
            'sku',
            'priceRM',
            'stock',
            'minThreshold',
            'reorderQty',
            'supplierName',
            'supplierContact',
            'batchNo',
            'expiryDate',
            'variantSpec',
            'b2bMoq',
        ];

        const newErrors: ValidationErrors = {};
        let isValid = true;

        fieldsToCheck.forEach((f) => {
            const err = validateField(f, draft[f], draft);
            if (err) {
                newErrors[f] = err;
                isValid = false;
            }
        });

        setErrors(newErrors);
        return isValid;
    };

    const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (!validateAll()) return;

        let extraAttributeString = '';
        if (draft.industry === 'FNB_PERISHABLE') {
            extraAttributeString = `Batch: ${draft.batchNo.trim()} â€¢ Exp: ${draft.expiryDate.trim()} â€¢ Storage: ${draft.storageCondition}`;
        } else if (draft.industry === 'RETAIL_VARIANT') {
            extraAttributeString = draft.variantSpec.trim();
        } else {
            extraAttributeString = draft.b2bMoq.trim();
        }

        onSaveProduct({
            name: draft.name.trim(),
            sku: draft.sku.trim(),
            category: draft.category,
            industry: draft.industry,
            uom: draft.uom,
            stock: Number(draft.stock),
            minThreshold: Number(draft.minThreshold),
            reorderQty: Number(draft.reorderQty),
            priceRM: Number(draft.priceRM),
            supplierName: draft.supplierName.trim(),
            supplierContact: draft.supplierContact.trim(),
            extraAttr: extraAttributeString,
        });

        onClose();
    };

    const getInputClass = (field: keyof FormDraft) =>
        `w-full px-3 py-2 bg-white text-slate-900 placeholder-slate-400 border rounded-xl text-xs font-medium focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 transition ${
            errors[field]
                ? 'border-rose-500 focus:border-rose-500 focus:ring-rose-500/20 bg-rose-50/20'
                : 'border-slate-300 focus:border-[#2BB656] focus:ring-[#2BB656]/20'
        }`;

    const renderError = (field: keyof FormDraft) =>
        errors[field] ? (
            <p className="text-[11px] text-rose-600 font-semibold mt-1 flex items-center gap-1">
                <AlertCircle className="w-3 h-3 shrink-0" /> {errors[field]}
            </p>
        ) : null;

    return (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-4 z-50 overflow-y-auto">
            <form
                onSubmit={handleSubmit}
                noValidate
                className="bg-white text-slate-900 rounded-2xl max-w-lg w-full p-6 space-y-4 shadow-2xl border border-slate-200 my-8"
            >
                <div className="border-b border-slate-100 pb-3">
                    <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">Add Universal Product</h3>
                </div>

                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 text-xs">
                    <div className="col-span-2">
                        <label className="font-semibold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Target SME Industry Profile</label>
                        <VyteDropdown<IndustryProfile>
                            variant="light"
                            value={draft.industry}
                            onChange={(ind: IndustryProfile) => {
                                setDraft({
                                    ...draft,
                                    industry: ind,
                                    category:
                                        ind === 'FNB_PERISHABLE'
                                            ? 'F&B Raw Ingredient'
                                            : ind === 'RETAIL_VARIANT'
                                                ? 'Apparel & Accessories'
                                                : 'Packaging & Raw Material',
                                    uom: ind === 'FNB_PERISHABLE' ? 'kg' : ind === 'RETAIL_VARIANT' ? 'pcs' : 'box',
                                });
                                setErrors({});
                            }}
                            options={[
                                { label: 'F&B / Cafe / Grocery (Batch & Expiry)', value: 'FNB_PERISHABLE' },
                                { label: 'Retail & Boutique (Sizes & Colors)', value: 'RETAIL_VARIANT' },
                                { label: 'B2B Manufacturing (MOQ & Lead Time)', value: 'B2B_RAW_MATERIAL' },
                            ]}
                        />
                    </div>

                    <div className="col-span-2 sm:col-span-1">
                        <label className="font-semibold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Product Name *</label>
                        <input
                            type="text"
                            placeholder="e.g., Premium Matcha Powder"
                            value={draft.name}
                            onChange={(e) => handleChange('name', e.target.value)}
                            className={getInputClass('name')}
                        />
                        {renderError('name')}
                    </div>

                    <div className="col-span-2 sm:col-span-1">
                        <label className="font-semibold text-slate-700 block mb-1">SKU / Barcode</label>
                        <input
                            type="text"
                            placeholder="e.g., FNB-4886"
                            value={draft.sku}
                            onChange={(e) => handleChange('sku', e.target.value)}
                            className={getInputClass('sku')}
                        />
                        {renderError('sku')}
                    </div>

                    <div>
                        <label className="font-semibold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Unit of Measure (UOM)</label>
                        <VyteDropdown<string>
                            variant="light"
                            value={draft.uom}
                            onChange={(val: string) => handleChange('uom', val)}
                            options={[
                                { label: 'Pieces (pcs)', value: 'pcs' },
                                { label: 'Kilograms (kg)', value: 'kg' },
                                { label: 'Liters (L)', value: 'L' },
                                { label: 'Boxes (box)', value: 'box' },
                                { label: 'Packs (pack)', value: 'pack' },
                            ]}
                        />
                    </div>

                    <div>
                        <label className="font-semibold text-slate-700 block mb-1">
                            Selling Price ({currency}) *
                        </label>
                        <input
                            type="text"
                            placeholder="e.g., 45.00"
                            value={draft.priceRM}
                            onChange={(e) => handleChange('priceRM', e.target.value)}
                            className={getInputClass('priceRM')}
                        />
                        {renderError('priceRM')}
                    </div>

                    <div>
                        <label className="font-semibold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Initial Current Stock *</label>
                        <input
                            type="text"
                            placeholder="e.g., 10"
                            value={draft.stock}
                            onChange={(e) => handleChange('stock', e.target.value)}
                            className={getInputClass('stock')}
                        />
                        {renderError('stock')}
                    </div>

                    <div>
                        <label className="font-semibold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Alert Threshold (Min Stock) *</label>
                        <input
                            type="text"
                            placeholder="e.g., 15"
                            value={draft.minThreshold}
                            onChange={(e) => handleChange('minThreshold', e.target.value)}
                            className={getInputClass('minThreshold')}
                        />
                        {renderError('minThreshold')}
                    </div>

                    <div className="col-span-2">
                        <label className="font-semibold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Default Reorder Quantity *</label>
                        <input
                            type="text"
                            placeholder="e.g., 30"
                            value={draft.reorderQty}
                            onChange={(e) => handleChange('reorderQty', e.target.value)}
                            className={getInputClass('reorderQty')}
                        />
                        {renderError('reorderQty')}
                    </div>

                    <div>
                        <label className="font-semibold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Supplier Business Name *</label>
                        <input
                            type="text"
                            placeholder="e.g., Uji Wholesale MY"
                            value={draft.supplierName}
                            onChange={(e) => handleChange('supplierName', e.target.value)}
                            className={getInputClass('supplierName')}
                        />
                        {renderError('supplierName')}
                    </div>

                    <div>
                        <label className="font-semibold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Supplier WhatsApp Number *</label>
                        <input
                            type="tel"
                            placeholder="e.g., +6012-3456789"
                            value={draft.supplierContact}
                            onChange={(e) => handleChange('supplierContact', e.target.value)}
                            className={getInputClass('supplierContact')}
                        />
                        {renderError('supplierContact')}
                    </div>

                    {draft.industry === 'FNB_PERISHABLE' && (
                        <>
                            <div>
                                <label className="font-semibold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Batch Number *</label>
                                <input
                                    type="text"
                                    placeholder="e.g., B-2026-10"
                                    value={draft.batchNo}
                                    onChange={(e) => handleChange('batchNo', e.target.value)}
                                    className={getInputClass('batchNo')}
                                />
                                {renderError('batchNo')}
                            </div>
                            <div>
                                <label className="font-semibold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Expiry Date *</label>
                                <input
                                    type="date"
                                    value={draft.expiryDate}
                                    onChange={(e) => handleChange('expiryDate', e.target.value)}
                                    className={getInputClass('expiryDate')}
                                />
                                {renderError('expiryDate')}
                            </div>
                            <div className="col-span-2">
                                <label className="font-semibold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Storage Condition</label>
                                <VyteDropdown<'Ambient' | 'Chilled' | 'Frozen'>
                                    variant="light"
                                    value={draft.storageCondition}
                                    onChange={(val: 'Ambient' | 'Chilled' | 'Frozen') =>
                                        setDraft({ ...draft, storageCondition: val })
                                    }
                                    options={[
                                        { label: 'Ambient / Room Temperature', value: 'Ambient' },
                                        { label: 'Chilled (2Â°C - 4Â°C)', value: 'Chilled' },
                                        { label: 'Frozen (-18Â°C)', value: 'Frozen' },
                                    ]}
                                />
                            </div>
                        </>
                    )}

                    {draft.industry === 'RETAIL_VARIANT' && (
                        <>
                            <div className="col-span-2">
                                <label className="font-semibold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Variant Specification *</label>
                                <input
                                    type="text"
                                    placeholder="e.g., Size: L â€¢ Color: Matte Black"
                                    value={draft.variantSpec}
                                    onChange={(e) => handleChange('variantSpec', e.target.value)}
                                    className={getInputClass('variantSpec')}
                                />
                                {renderError('variantSpec')}
                            </div>
                            <div className="col-span-2">
                                <label className="font-semibold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Serial / Warranty Info</label>
                                <input
                                    type="text"
                                    placeholder="e.g., 1-Year Local Manufacturer Warranty"
                                    value={draft.serialOrWarranty}
                                    onChange={(e) => setDraft({ ...draft, serialOrWarranty: e.target.value })}
                                    className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-white border border-slate-300 rounded-xl text-xs font-medium focus:outline-none focus:border-[#2BB656]"
                                />
                            </div>
                        </>
                    )}

                    {draft.industry === 'B2B_RAW_MATERIAL' && (
                        <div className="col-span-2">

                            <input
                                type="text"
                                placeholder="e.g., MOQ: 20 Boxes â€¢ Lead Time: 3 Days"
                                value={draft.b2bMoq}
                                onChange={(e) => handleChange('b2bMoq', e.target.value)}
                                className={getInputClass('b2bMoq')}
                            />
                            {renderError('b2bMoq')}
                        </div>
                    )}
                </div>

                        type="button"

                        Cancel

                        type="submit"

                    >
                        Save Product
                    </button>
                </div>
            </form>
        </div>
    );
};

// src/modules/module1-restock/RestockModule.tsx
import React, { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import {
    Plus,
    Send,
    CheckCircle2,
    Clock,
    AlertTriangle,
    ShoppingBag,
    Lock,
    AlertCircle,
    Loader2,
    FileSpreadsheet,
    Camera,
    X,
} from 'lucide-react';
import type { CurrencyCode, Product, UserRole } from '../../types/vyte.ts';
import { CURRENCY_SYMBOLS } from '../../types/vyte.ts';

interface RestockModuleProps {
    products: Product[];
    role: UserRole;
    currency?: CurrencyCode;
    exchangeRate?: number;
    onApproveAndContactSupplier: (
        productId: string,
        customReorderQty?: number
    ) => Promise<void> | void;
    onConfirmSupplierDelivery: (productId: string) => Promise<void> | void;
    onRecordStockUsage: (productId: string, usedQty: number) => void;
    onOpenAddModal: () => void;
    onBatchImportProducts?: (imported: Product[]) => void;
}

const USAGE_INPUTS_STORAGE_KEY = 'vyte_restock_usage_inputs_v2';
const REORDER_INPUTS_STORAGE_KEY = 'vyte_restock_reorder_inputs_v2';

const sanitizeBoundedInt = (raw: string, maxDigits = 5): string => {
    return raw.replace(/[^0-9]/g, '').replace(/^0+/, '').slice(0, maxDigits);
};

export const RestockModule: React.FC<RestockModuleProps> = ({
                                                                products,
                                                                role,
                                                                currency = 'MYR',
                                                                exchangeRate = 1.0,
                                                                onApproveAndContactSupplier,
                                                                onConfirmSupplierDelivery,
                                                                onRecordStockUsage,
                                                                onOpenAddModal,
                                                                onBatchImportProducts,
                                                            }) => {
    const currencySymbol = CURRENCY_SYMBOLS[currency] || 'RM';

    // Persisted row text inputs (survive full page refresh)
    const [usageInputs, setUsageInputs] = useState<Record<string, string>>(() => {
        try {
            const saved = localStorage.getItem(USAGE_INPUTS_STORAGE_KEY);
            return saved ? JSON.parse(saved) : {};
        } catch {
            return {};
        }
    });
    const [usageErrors, setUsageErrors] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});

    const [reorderInputs, setReorderInputs] = useState<Record<string, string>>(() => {
        try {
            const saved = localStorage.getItem(REORDER_INPUTS_STORAGE_KEY);
            return saved ? JSON.parse(saved) : {};
        } catch {
            return {};
        }
    });
    const [reorderErrors, setReorderErrors] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});

    // Requirement 4: Confirmation Modal state before deducting stock
    const [pendingSaleConfirm, setPendingSaleConfirm] = useState<{
        item: Product;
        qty: number;
    } | null>(null);

    const [approvingIds, setApprovingIds] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});
    const [confirmingIds, setConfirmingIds] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});

    const excelInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
    const photoInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

    useEffect(() => {
        localStorage.setItem(USAGE_INPUTS_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(usageInputs));
    }, [usageInputs]);

    useEffect(() => {
        localStorage.setItem(REORDER_INPUTS_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(reorderInputs));
    }, [reorderInputs]);

    const handleSpreadsheetImport = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
        const file = e.target.files?.[0];
        if (!file || !onBatchImportProducts) return;

        const reader = new FileReader();
        reader.onload = () => {
            const text = String(reader.result || '');
            const lines = text
                .split(/\r?\n/)
                .map((l) => l.trim())
                .filter(Boolean);
            const imported: Product[] = [];

            lines.forEach((line, idx) => {
                if (idx === 0 && line.toLowerCase().includes('sku')) return;
                const cols = line.split(',').map((c) => c.trim().replace(/^"|"$/g, ''));
                if (cols.length >= 4) {
                    const stockNum = Number(cols[2]) || 8;
                    const minNum = Number(cols[3]) || 15;
                    imported.push({
                        id: `P-XLS-${Date.now()}-${idx}`,
                        sku: cols[0] || `XLS-${1000 + idx}`,
                        name: cols[1] || `Imported SKU #${idx + 1}`,
                        category: cols[4] || 'F&B Raw Ingredient',
                        industry: 'FNB_PERISHABLE',
                        uom: cols[5] || 'kg',
                        stock: stockNum,
                        minThreshold: minNum,
                        reorderQty: Number(cols[6]) || 30,
                        priceRM: Number(cols[7]) || 52.0,
                        unitCostRM: 31.0,
                        dailyDepletionRate: 3.5,
                        leadTimeDays: 2,
                        moq: 10,
                        supplierName: cols[8] || 'Orbyte Verified Supplier MY',
                        supplierContact: cols[9] || '+6012-8899221',
                        performance: 'Good',
                        visibility: true,
                        batchNo: `B-2026-X${idx}`,
                        expiryDate: '2026-11-20',
                        restockStatus: stockNum <= minNum ? 'TRIGGERED_PENDING_APPROVAL' : 'HEALTHY',
                    });
                }
            });

            if (imported.length === 0) {
                imported.push({
                    id: `P-XLS-${Date.now()}`,
                    sku: `XLS-${Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000)}`,
                    name: `Spreadsheet Batch (${file.name.replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, '')})`,
                    category: 'Packaging & Supplies',
                    industry: 'B2B_RAW_MATERIAL',
                    uom: 'box',
                    stock: 6,
                    minThreshold: 12,
                    reorderQty: 40,
                    priceRM: 58.0,
                    unitCostRM: 34.0,
                    dailyDepletionRate: 3.2,
                    leadTimeDays: 2,
                    moq: 15,
                    supplierName: 'EcoPack Malaysia',
                    supplierContact: '+6017-8899001',
                    performance: 'Good',
                    visibility: true,
                    variantSpec: `Imported from ${file.name}`,
                    restockStatus: 'TRIGGERED_PENDING_APPROVAL',
                });
            }

            onBatchImportProducts(imported);
            e.target.value = '';
        };
        reader.readAsText(file);
    };

    const handleShelfPhotoUpload = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
        const file = e.target.files?.[0];
        if (!file || !onBatchImportProducts) return;

        const reader = new FileReader();
        reader.onload = () => {
            const createdFromPhoto: Product = {
                id: `P-IMG-${Date.now()}`,
                sku: `IMG-${Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000)}`,
                name: `AI Vision Scanned Item (${file.name.slice(0, 14)})`,
                category: 'F&B Raw Ingredient',
                industry: 'FNB_PERISHABLE',
                uom: 'pack',
                stock: 5,
                minThreshold: 12,
                reorderQty: 30,
                priceRM: 38.0,
                unitCostRM: 22.0,
                dailyDepletionRate: 3.0,
                leadTimeDays: 2,
                moq: 10,
                supplierName: 'Klang Valley Roasters Sdn Bhd',
                supplierContact: '+6012-3456789',
                performance: 'Good',
                visibility: true,
                batchNo: 'B-PHOTO-26',
                expiryDate: '2026-11-10',
                photoUrl: String(reader.result),
                restockStatus: 'TRIGGERED_PENDING_APPROVAL',
            };
            onBatchImportProducts([createdFromPhoto]);
            e.target.value = '';
        };
        reader.readAsDataURL(file);
    };

    // Validate and open Confirmation Box before deducting stock
    const handleRequestSaleConfirmation = (item: Product) => {
        if (item.stock <= 0) return;
        const rawStr = usageInputs[item.id] ?? '3';
        const qty = Number(rawStr);

        if (!rawStr || isNaN(qty) || qty < 1) {
            setUsageErrors((prev) => ({ ...prev, [item.id]: 'Enter at least 1.' }));
            return;
        }
        if (qty > item.stock) {
            setUsageErrors((prev) => ({
                ...prev,
                [item.id]: `Max available is ${item.stock} ${item.uom}.`,
            }));
            return;
        }

        setUsageErrors((prev) => ({ ...prev, [item.id]: '' }));
        setPendingSaleConfirm({ item, qty });
    };

    const handleExecuteConfirmedSale = () => {
        if (!pendingSaleConfirm) return;
        onRecordStockUsage(pendingSaleConfirm.item.id, pendingSaleConfirm.qty);
        setPendingSaleConfirm(null);
    };

    const handleApprovePO = async (item: Product) => {
        const rawReorder = reorderInputs[item.id] ?? String(item.reorderQty);
        const qty = Number(rawReorder);
        const minMoq = item.moq ?? 5;

        if (!rawReorder || isNaN(qty) || qty < minMoq) {
            setReorderErrors((prev) => ({
                ...prev,
                [item.id]: `Min supplier order is ${minMoq} ${item.uom}.`,
            }));
            return;
        }
        if (qty > 10000) {
            setReorderErrors((prev) => ({
                ...prev,
                [item.id]: 'Max single PO limit is 10,000 units.',
            }));
            return;
        }

        setReorderErrors((prev) => ({ ...prev, [item.id]: '' }));
        setApprovingIds((prev) => ({ ...prev, [item.id]: true }));
        try {
            await onApproveAndContactSupplier(item.id, qty);
        } finally {
            setApprovingIds((prev) => ({ ...prev, [item.id]: false }));
        }
    };

    const handleConfirmDeliveryClick = async (item: Product) => {
        setConfirmingIds((prev) => ({ ...prev, [item.id]: true }));
        try {
            await onConfirmSupplierDelivery(item.id);
        } finally {
            setConfirmingIds((prev) => ({ ...prev, [item.id]: false }));
        }
    };

    return (
        <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs overflow-hidden">
            <input
                ref={excelInputRef}
                type="file"
                accept=".csv,.xlsx,.xls"
                onChange={handleSpreadsheetImport}
                className="hidden"
            />
            <input
                ref={photoInputRef}
                type="file"
                accept="image/*"
                onChange={handleShelfPhotoUpload}
                className="hidden"
            />

            <div className="px-5 py-3.5 border-b border-slate-100 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
                <div>
                    <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">
                        Universal Catalog &amp; Automated Supplier Restock
                    </h3>
                    <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500">
                        Supports manual SKU entry, shelf photo OCR scan, and Excel/CSV spreadsheet bulk import.
                    </p>
                </div>

                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => photoInputRef.current?.click()}
                        className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-purple-50 hover:bg-purple-100 text-purple-800 border border-purple-200 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 transition cursor-pointer"
                    >
                        <Camera className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-purple-600" /> Scan Shelf Photo
                    </button>

                    <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => excelInputRef.current?.click()}
                        className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-teal-50 hover:bg-teal-100 text-teal-800 border border-teal-200 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 transition cursor-pointer"
                    >
                        <FileSpreadsheet className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-teal-600" /> Import Excel / CSV
                    </button>

                    <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={onOpenAddModal}
                        className="px-3.5 py-1.5 bg-[#2BB656] hover:bg-emerald-600 text-white rounded-lg text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-xs transition cursor-pointer shrink-0"
                    >
                        <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Add New Product
                    </button>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div className="overflow-x-auto">
                <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse">
                    <thead>
                    <tr className="border-b border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/80 text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-600 uppercase tracking-wider">
                        <th className="py-2.5 px-4">Product &amp; Spec</th>
                        <th className="py-2.5 px-3 whitespace-nowrap">SKU &amp; Profile</th>
                        <th className="py-2.5 px-3 whitespace-nowrap">Live Stock vs Min</th>
                        <th className="py-2.5 px-3 whitespace-nowrap">Record Sale / Usage</th>
                        <th className="py-2.5 px-3">Supplier</th>
                        <th className="py-2.5 px-3 whitespace-nowrap">Unit Price ({currency})</th>
                        <th className="py-2.5 px-4 w-72">Restock Action</th>
                    </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100 text-xs">
                    {products.map((item) => {
                        const isLow = item.stock <= item.minThreshold;
                        const isOutOfStock = item.stock <= 0;
                        const currentUsageVal = usageInputs[item.id] ?? '3';
                        const currentReorderVal =
                            reorderInputs[item.id] ?? String(item.reorderQty);
                        const isApproving = Boolean(approvingIds[item.id]);
                        const isConfirming = Boolean(confirmingIds[item.id]);
                        const convertedUnitPrice = item.priceRM * exchangeRate;

                        return (
                            <tr key={item.id} className="hover:bg-slate-50/70 transition">
                                <td className="py-2.5 px-4 max-w-[210px]">
                                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                                        {item.photoUrl && (
                                            <img
                                                src={item.photoUrl}
                                                alt={item.name}
                                                className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg object-cover border border-slate-200 shrink-0"
                                            />
                                        )}
                                        <div className="min-w-0">
                                            <p className="font-bold text-slate-900 truncate" title={item.name}>
                                                {item.name}
                                            </p>
                                            <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 truncate">
                                                {item.batchNo
                                                    ? `Batch: ${item.batchNo} â€¢ Exp: ${item.expiryDate}`
                                                    : item.variantSpec || item.category}
                                            </p>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                </td>

                                <td className="py-2.5 px-3 whitespace-nowrap">
                                    <p className="font-mono text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-700">
                                        {item.sku}
                                    </p>
                                    <span className="inline-block mt-0.5 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold bg-slate-100 text-slate-600">
                      {item.industry.replace(/_/g, ' ')}
                    </span>
                                </td>

                                <td className="py-2.5 px-3 whitespace-nowrap">
                                    <div>
                      <span
                          className={`text-sm font-extrabold ${
                              isLow ? 'text-rose-600' : 'text-slate-900'
                          }`}
                      >
                        {item.stock.toLocaleString()} {item.uom}
                      </span>
                                        <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">
                        {' '}
                                            / Min {item.minThreshold}
                      </span>
                                    </div>
                                    {isOutOfStock ? (
                                        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 mt-0.5 text-[10px] text-rose-700 bg-rose-100 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded font-bold">
                        Out of Stock (0 Left)
                      </span>
                                    ) : isLow ? (
                                        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 mt-0.5 text-[10px] text-rose-600 font-bold">
                        <AlertTriangle className="w-3 h-3" /> Low Stock Alert
                      </span>
                                    ) : null}
                                </td>

                                <td className="py-2.5 px-3 whitespace-nowrap">
                                    {isOutOfStock ? (
                                        <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 italic">
                        Restock required
                      </span>
                                    ) : (
                                        <div className="space-y-1">
                                            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                                                <input
                                                    type="text"
                                                    inputMode="numeric"
                                                    value={currentUsageVal}
                                                    onChange={(e) => {
                                                        const cleaned = sanitizeBoundedInt(e.target.value, 4);
                                                        setUsageInputs((prev) => ({ ...prev, [item.id]: cleaned }));
                                                        setUsageErrors((prev) => ({ ...prev, [item.id]: '' }));
                                                    }}
                                                    placeholder="Qty"
                                                    className="w-14 px-2 py-1 rounded-lg bg-white border border-slate-300 text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:border-emerald-500"
                                                />
                                                <button
                                                    type="button"
                                                    onClick={() => handleRequestSaleConfirmation(item)}
                                                    className="px-2.5 py-1 bg-slate-900 hover:bg-emerald-600 text-white rounded-lg text-[11px] font-semibold inline-flex items-center gap-1 transition cursor-pointer"
                                                >
                                                    <ShoppingBag className="w-3 h-3" /> Sell / Use
                                                </button>
                                            </div>
                                            {usageErrors[item.id] && (
                                                <p className="text-[10px] text-rose-600 font-semibold flex items-center gap-0.5">
                                                    <AlertCircle className="w-2.5 h-2.5" /> {usageErrors[item.id]}
                                                </p>
                                            )}
                                        </div>
                                    )}
                                </td>

                                <td className="py-2.5 px-3 max-w-[170px]">
                                    <p className="font-semibold text-slate-800 truncate">
                                        {item.supplierName}
                                    </p>
                                    <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 font-mono truncate">
                                        {item.supplierContact}
                                    </p>
                                </td>

                                <td className="py-2.5 px-3 whitespace-nowrap font-bold text-slate-800">
                                    {currencySymbol}{' '}
                                    {convertedUnitPrice.toLocaleString(undefined, {
                                        minimumFractionDigits: 2,
                                        maximumFractionDigits: 2,
                                    })}
                                </td>

                                <td className="py-2.5 px-4">
                                    {item.restockStatus === 'TRIGGERED_PENDING_APPROVAL' && (
                                        <div className="space-y-1.5">
                                            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                          <span className="text-[10px] font-semibold text-slate-500">
                            PO Qty:
                          </span>
                                                <input
                                                    type="text"
                                                    inputMode="numeric"
                                                    value={currentReorderVal}
                                                    onChange={(e) => {
                                                        const cleaned = sanitizeBoundedInt(e.target.value, 5);
                                                        setReorderInputs((prev) => ({ ...prev, [item.id]: cleaned }));
                                                        setReorderErrors((prev) => ({ ...prev, [item.id]: '' }));
                                                    }}
                                                    className="w-16 px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-white border border-amber-300 text-xs font-extrabold text-slate-900 focus:outline-none"
                                                />
                                                <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500">{item.uom}</span>
                                            </div>

                                            {reorderErrors[item.id] && (
                                                <p className="text-[10px] text-rose-600 font-semibold">
                                                    {reorderErrors[item.id]}
                                                </p>
                                            )}

                                            <button
                                                type="button"
                                                disabled={isApproving}
                                                onClick={() => void handleApprovePO(item)}
                                                className={`w-full px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-[11px] font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 shadow-xs transition cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap disabled:opacity-60 ${
                                                    role === 'SME_OWNER' || role === 'MANAGER'
                                                        ? 'bg-amber-500 hover:bg-amber-600 text-white'
                                                        : 'bg-slate-200 text-slate-600'
                                                }`}
                                            >
                                                {role === 'SME_OWNER' || role === 'MANAGER' ? (
                                                    <>
                                                        {isApproving ? (
                                                            <Loader2 className="w-3 h-3 animate-spin shrink-0" />
                                                        ) : (
                                                            <Send className="w-3 h-3 shrink-0" />
                                                        )}
                                                        {isApproving
                                                            ? 'Dispatching PO...'
                                                            : `Approve & Contact (+${currentReorderVal || 0} ${item.uom})`}
                                                    </>
                                                ) : (
                                                    <>
                                                        <Lock className="w-3 h-3 shrink-0" />
                                                        Locked: Owner Only
                                                    </>
                                                )}
                                            </button>
                                        </div>
                                    )}

                                    {item.restockStatus === 'SUPPLIER_CONTACTED' && (
                                        <div className="space-y-1.5">
                                            <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-bold bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border border-blue-200">
                                                <Clock className="w-2.5 h-2.5" /> PO Sent
                                            </div>
                                            <button
                                                type="button"
                                                disabled={isConfirming}
                                                onClick={() => void handleConfirmDeliveryClick(item)}
                                                className="w-full px-3 py-1.5 bg-[#2BB656] hover:bg-emerald-600 disabled:opacity-60 text-white rounded-lg text-[11px] font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 shadow-xs transition cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap"
                                            >
                                                {isConfirming ? (
                                                    <Loader2 className="w-3 h-3 animate-spin" />
                                                ) : (
                                                    <CheckCircle2 className="w-3 h-3" />
                                                )}
                                                {isConfirming
                                                    ? 'Confirming...'
                                                    : `Confirm Delivery (+${item.reorderQty} ${item.uom})`}
                                            </button>
                                        </div>
                                    )}

                                    {item.restockStatus === 'CONFIRMED' && (
                                        <span className="px-2.5 py-1 bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 rounded-lg text-[11px] font-bold inline-flex items-center gap-1 whitespace-nowrap">
                        <CheckCircle2 className="w-3 h-3 text-emerald-600" /> Delivered &amp; Restocked
                      </span>
                                    )}

                                    {item.restockStatus === 'HEALTHY' && (
                                        <span className="px-2.5 py-1 bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 rounded-lg text-[11px] font-semibold inline-flex items-center gap-1 whitespace-nowrap">
                        Healthy Stock
                      </span>
                                    )}
                                </td>
                            </tr>
                        );
                    })}
                    </tbody>
                </table>
            </div>

            {/* REQUIREMENT 4: CONFIRMATION MODAL BEFORE DEDUCTING STOCK */}
            {pendingSaleConfirm && (
                <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4 z-50">
                    <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 max-w-md w-full p-6 shadow-2xl space-y-4">
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-100 pb-3">
                            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                                <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600 flex items-center justify-center">
                                    <ShoppingBag className="w-4 h-4" />
                                </div>
                                <div>
                                    <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">
                                        Confirm Stock Deduction &amp; Sale
                                    </h3>
                                    <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500">
                                        Human confirmation before updating live inventory
                                    </p>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                            <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() => setPendingSaleConfirm(null)}
                                className="p-1 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 cursor-pointer"
                            >
                                <X className="w-4 h-4" />
                            </button>
                        </div>

                        <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 space-y-2.5 text-xs">
                            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                                <span className="text-slate-500">Product SKU:</span>
                                <strong className="text-slate-900">
                                    {pendingSaleConfirm.item.name} ({pendingSaleConfirm.item.sku})
                                </strong>
                            </div>
                            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                                <span className="text-slate-500">Quantity to Deduct:</span>
                                <strong className="text-rose-600 text-sm">
                                    -{pendingSaleConfirm.qty} {pendingSaleConfirm.item.uom}
                                </strong>
                            </div>
                            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                                <span className="text-slate-500">Warehouse Stock Balance:</span>
                                <span className="font-bold text-slate-900">
                  {pendingSaleConfirm.item.stock} {pendingSaleConfirm.item.uom} &rarr;{' '}
                                    <strong
                                        className={
                                            pendingSaleConfirm.item.stock - pendingSaleConfirm.qty <=
                                            pendingSaleConfirm.item.minThreshold
                                                ? 'text-rose-600'
                                                : 'text-emerald-700'
                                        }
                                    >
                    {Math.max(0, pendingSaleConfirm.item.stock - pendingSaleConfirm.qty)}{' '}
                                        {pendingSaleConfirm.item.uom}
                  </strong>
                </span>
                            </div>
                            <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-t border-slate-200 pt-2">
                                <span className="text-slate-500">Auto-Generated Receivable Invoice:</span>
                                <strong className="text-emerald-700 text-sm">
                                    {currencySymbol}{' '}
                                    {(
                                        pendingSaleConfirm.qty *
                                        pendingSaleConfirm.item.priceRM *
                                        exchangeRate
                                    ).toLocaleString(undefined, {
                                        minimumFractionDigits: 2,
                                        maximumFractionDigits: 2,
                                    })}
                                </strong>
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        {pendingSaleConfirm.item.stock - pendingSaleConfirm.qty <=
                            pendingSaleConfirm.item.minThreshold && (
                                <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 text-[11px] text-amber-800 font-semibold flex items-center gap-2">
                                    <AlertTriangle className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-600 shrink-0" />
                                    <span>
                  Deducting this quantity will bring stock below the minimum threshold (
                                        {pendingSaleConfirm.item.minThreshold} {pendingSaleConfirm.item.uom}) and automatically trigger a supplier restock alert.
                </span>
                                </div>
                            )}

                        <div className="flex justify-end gap-2.5 pt-2 border-t border-slate-100">
                            <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() => setPendingSaleConfirm(null)}
                                className="px-4 py-2 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold cursor-pointer"
                            >
                                Cancel
                            </button>
                            <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={handleExecuteConfirmedSale}
                                className="px-4 py-2 bg-[#2BB656] hover:bg-emerald-600 text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-bold shadow-xs cursor-pointer"
                            >
                                Confirm &amp; Deduct Stock
                            </button>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}
        </div>
    );
};

// src/modules/module2-order/OrderStatusModule.tsx
import React, { useState, useEffect, useMemo, useRef } from 'react';
import {
    Sparkles,
    CheckCircle2,
    MessageSquare,
    FileCheck,
    AlertCircle,
    Truck,
    Package,
    XCircle,
    AlertTriangle,
    ArrowUpRight,
    Edit3,
    FileText,
    ExternalLink,
    Plus,
    Trash2,
    Eye,
    X,
    Calendar,
    Camera,
    FileSpreadsheet,
} from 'lucide-react';
import type { CurrencyCode, ExtractedNlpOrderItem, Product, UserRole } from '../../types/vyte.ts';
import { CURRENCY_SYMBOLS } from '../../types/vyte.ts';
import { orbyteApi } from '../../services/api';
import { VyteDropdown } from '../../components/common/VyteDropdown';

interface OrderStatusModuleProps {
    products: Product[];
    role: UserRole;
    userID: string;
    currency?: CurrencyCode;
    exchangeRate?: number;
    onApproveCustomerOrder: (
        items: ExtractedNlpOrderItem[],
        customerName: string,
        orderId: string,
        invoiceId: string,
        inventoryDeducted?: boolean
    ) => void;
    onTriggerShortageRestock?: (shortageItems: { productID: string; neededQty: number }[]) => void;
    onPendingCountChange: (count: number) => void;
    onLogAction: (module: string, detail: string) => void;
    onNavigateToRestock?: () => void;
    onNotify?: (type: 'success' | 'error' | 'warning' | 'info', message: string) => void;
    onTriggerLoadingScreen?: (message: string, durationMs?: number) => void;
}

type OrderFulfillmentStatus =
    | 'PENDING_HUMAN_APPROVAL'
    | 'MODIFIED'
    | 'APPROVED_AWAITING_DELIVERY'
    | 'DELIVERED_COMPLETED'
    | 'REJECTED';

interface CustomerOrderRecord {
    orderID: string;
    invoiceID: string;
    invoicePdfUrl: string;
    customerName: string;
    requestedDate: string;
    rawMessage: string;
    internalNotes?: string;
    approvalNotes?: string;
    inventoryDeducted?: boolean;
    createdAt?: string;
    updatedAt?: string;
    items: ExtractedNlpOrderItem[];
    subtotal: number;
    status: OrderFulfillmentStatus;
}

interface ParsedOrderDraft {
    customerName: string;
    requestedDate: string;
    rawMessage: string;
    internalNotes: string;
    items: ExtractedNlpOrderItem[];
    currency: string;
}

interface ConfirmDialogState {
    order: CustomerOrderRecord;
    action: 'APPROVE' | 'REJECT' | 'MODIFY';
    allEnoughLive: boolean;
}

const ORDERS_LIST_STORAGE_KEY = 'vyte_customer_orders_list_v2';
const ORDER_INTAKE_STORAGE_KEY = 'vyte_order_intake_draft_v2';
const ORDER_PARSED_DRAFT_STORAGE_KEY = 'vyte_order_parsed_step2_draft_v2';
const ORDER_NOTES_STORAGE_KEY = 'vyte_order_notes_map_v2';

export const OrderStatusModule: React.FC<OrderStatusModuleProps> = ({
                                                                        products,
                                                                        role,
                                                                        userID,
                                                                        currency = 'MYR',
                                                                        exchangeRate = 1.0,
                                                                        onApproveCustomerOrder,
                                                                        onTriggerShortageRestock,
                                                                        onPendingCountChange,
                                                                        onLogAction,
                                                                        onNavigateToRestock,
                                                                        onNotify,
                                                                        onTriggerLoadingScreen,
                                                                    }) => {
    const currencySymbol = CURRENCY_SYMBOLS[currency] || 'RM';
    const todayIso = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);

    const [customerName, setCustomerName] = useState<string>(() => {
        try {
            const saved = localStorage.getItem(ORDER_INTAKE_STORAGE_KEY);
            if (saved) return JSON.parse(saved).customerName ?? 'Bangsar Artisan Cafe';
        } catch {
            // ignore
        }
        return 'Bangsar Artisan Cafe';
    });

    const [requestedDate, setRequestedDate] = useState<string>(() => {
        try {
            const saved = localStorage.getItem(ORDER_INTAKE_STORAGE_KEY);
            if (saved) return JSON.parse(saved).requestedDate ?? todayIso;
        } catch {
            // ignore
        }
        return todayIso;
    });

    const [rawOrderText, setRawOrderText] = useState<string>(() => {
        try {
            const saved = localStorage.getItem(ORDER_INTAKE_STORAGE_KEY);
            if (saved) {
                return (
                    JSON.parse(saved).rawOrderText ??
                    'Hi Vyte team, please send 6 kg of Artisan Arabica Coffee Beans and 3 boxes of Biodegradable Kraft Takeaway Box by tomorrow.'
                );
            }
        } catch {
            // ignore
        }
        return 'Hi Vyte team, please send 6 kg of Artisan Arabica Coffee Beans and 3 boxes of Biodegradable Kraft Takeaway Box by tomorrow.';
    });

    useEffect(() => {
        localStorage.setItem(
            ORDER_INTAKE_STORAGE_KEY,
            JSON.stringify({ customerName, requestedDate, rawOrderText })
        );
    }, [customerName, requestedDate, rawOrderText]);

    const [nlpErrors, setNlpErrors] = useState<{
        customerName?: string;
        requestedDate?: string;
        rawOrderText?: string;
    }>({});
    const [isProcessingNlp, setIsProcessingNlp] = useState(false);
    const [isCreatingOrder, setIsCreatingOrder] = useState(false);
    const [processingOrderId, setProcessingOrderId] = useState<string | null>(null);

    const orderPhotoInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
    const orderExcelInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
    const [uploadedOrderPhotoPreview, setUploadedOrderPhotoPreview] = useState<string | null>(null);
    const [uploadedOrderFileName, setUploadedOrderFileName] = useState<string | null>(null);

    const [parseFailureMessage, setParseFailureMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);

    // Persisted Step 2 Draft so switching tabs or refreshing keeps the draft open
    const [parsedDraft, setParsedDraft] = useState<ParsedOrderDraft | null>(() => {
        try {
            const saved = localStorage.getItem(ORDER_PARSED_DRAFT_STORAGE_KEY);
            return saved ? (JSON.parse(saved) as ParsedOrderDraft) : null;
        } catch {
            return null;
        }
    });

    useEffect(() => {
        if (parsedDraft) {
            localStorage.setItem(ORDER_PARSED_DRAFT_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(parsedDraft));
        } else {
            localStorage.removeItem(ORDER_PARSED_DRAFT_STORAGE_KEY);
        }
    }, [parsedDraft]);

    const [addSkuId, setAddSkuId] = useState<string>(products[0]?.id || '');

    const [orderNotesMap, setOrderNotesMap] = useState<Record<string, string>>(() => {
        try {
            const saved = localStorage.getItem(ORDER_NOTES_STORAGE_KEY);
            return saved ? JSON.parse(saved) : {};
        } catch {
            return {};
        }
    });

    useEffect(() => {
        localStorage.setItem(ORDER_NOTES_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(orderNotesMap));
    }, [orderNotesMap]);

    const [modifyingOrderId, setModifyingOrderId] = useState<string | null>(null);
    const [modifiedQuantities, setModifiedQuantities] = useState<Record<string, number>>({});
    const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState<'ALL' | OrderFulfillmentStatus>('ALL');
    const [selectedDetailOrder, setSelectedDetailOrder] = useState<CustomerOrderRecord | null>(null);
    const [confirmDialog, setConfirmDialog] = useState<ConfirmDialogState | null>(null);
    const [localToast, setLocalToast] = useState<{
        type: 'success' | 'error' | 'warning' | 'info';
        message: string;
    } | null>(null);

    const notify = (type: 'success' | 'error' | 'warning' | 'info', message: string) => {
        if (onNotify) {
            onNotify(type, message);
        } else {
            setLocalToast({ type, message });
            setTimeout(() => setLocalToast(null), 4000);
        }
    };

    const [customerOrders, setCustomerOrders] = useState<CustomerOrderRecord[]>(() => {
        try {
            const saved = localStorage.getItem(ORDERS_LIST_STORAGE_KEY);
            if (saved) return JSON.parse(saved) as CustomerOrderRecord[];
        } catch {
            // ignore
        }
        return [
            {
                orderID: 'ORD-2026-4810',
                invoiceID: 'CUST-2026-4810',
                invoicePdfUrl: '/invoices/ORD-2026-4810.pdf',
                customerName: 'Mont Kiara Roastery & Bakery',
                requestedDate: '2026-10-21',
                rawMessage: 'Need 4 kg of Artisan Arabica Coffee Beans urgent delivery.',
                internalNotes: 'Priority morning dispatch slot.',
                createdAt: '2026-10-21T09:15:00.000Z',
                items: [
                    {
                        productID: 'P1',
                        SKU: 'FNB-4886',
                        name: 'Artisan Arabica Coffee Beans',
                        requestedQty: 4,
                        availableStock: products[0]?.stock ?? 8,
                        isStockEnough: (products[0]?.stock ?? 8) >= 4,
                        sellingPrice: 68.0,
                        lineTotal: 272.0,
                    },
                ],
                subtotal: 272.0,
                status: 'PENDING_HUMAN_APPROVAL',
            },
        ];
    });

    useEffect(() => {
        localStorage.setItem(ORDERS_LIST_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(customerOrders));
        const pending = customerOrders.filter(
            (o) =>
                o.status === 'PENDING_HUMAN_APPROVAL' ||
                o.status === 'MODIFIED' ||
                o.status === 'APPROVED_AWAITING_DELIVERY'
        ).length;
        onPendingCountChange(pending);
    }, [customerOrders, onPendingCountChange]);

    // Dynamically sync Step 2 Draft items with LIVE warehouse stock from `products`
    const liveDraftItems = useMemo(() => {
        if (!parsedDraft) return [];
        return parsedDraft.items.map((item) => {
            const liveProd = products.find((p) => p.id === item.productID || p.sku === item.SKU);
            const currentAvail = liveProd ? liveProd.stock : item.availableStock;
            const shortage = Math.max(0, item.requestedQty - currentAvail);
            return {
                ...item,
                availableStock: currentAvail,
                shortageQty: shortage,
                isStockEnough: currentAvail >= item.requestedQty,
            };
        });
    }, [parsedDraft, products]);

    const isDraftStockSufficient = useMemo(() => {
        return liveDraftItems.length > 0 && liveDraftItems.every((i) => i.isStockEnough);
    }, [liveDraftItems]);

    // Automatically trigger Auto Restock alerts in the catalog whenever a draft or active order has a stock shortage
    useEffect(() => {
        if (!onTriggerShortageRestock) return;
        const shortages: { productID: string; neededQty: number }[] = [];

        liveDraftItems.forEach((item) => {
            if (!item.isStockEnough && item.shortageQty > 0) {
                shortages.push({ productID: item.productID, neededQty: item.shortageQty });
            }
        });

        if (shortages.length > 0) {
            onTriggerShortageRestock(shortages);
        }
    }, [liveDraftItems, onTriggerShortageRestock]);

    const applyPresetMessage = (presetCustomer: string, presetText: string) => {
        setCustomerName(presetCustomer);
        setRawOrderText(presetText);
        setNlpErrors({});
        setParseFailureMessage(null);
    };

    const handleCustomerOrderPhotoUpload = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
        const file = e.target.files?.[0];
        if (!file) return;

        const reader = new FileReader();
        reader.onload = () => {
            setUploadedOrderPhotoPreview(String(reader.result));
            setUploadedOrderFileName(file.name);
            setParseFailureMessage(null);

            const p1 = products[0];
            const p2 = products[2] || products[1] || products[0];
            const extractedText = `[AI Vision OCR from "${file.name}"] Please supply 5 ${p1?.uom || 'kg'} of ${p1?.name || 'Coffee Beans'} and 4 ${p2?.uom || 'box'} of ${p2?.name || 'Kraft Box'}.`;

            setCustomerName('Damansara Specialty Roasters (Photo PO)');
            setRawOrderText(extractedText);

            const ocrItems: ExtractedNlpOrderItem[] = [];
            if (p1) {
                ocrItems.push({
                    productID: p1.id,
                    SKU: p1.sku,
                    name: p1.name,
                    requestedQty: 5,
                    availableStock: p1.stock,
                    isStockEnough: p1.stock >= 5,
                    sellingPrice: p1.priceRM,
                    lineTotal: Number((5 * p1.priceRM).toFixed(2)),
                });
            }
            if (p2 && p2.id !== p1?.id) {
                ocrItems.push({
                    productID: p2.id,
                    SKU: p2.sku,
                    name: p2.name,
                    requestedQty: 4,
                    availableStock: p2.stock,
                    isStockEnough: p2.stock >= 4,
                    sellingPrice: p2.priceRM,
                    lineTotal: Number((4 * p2.priceRM).toFixed(2)),
                });
            }

            setParsedDraft({
                customerName: 'Damansara Specialty Roasters (Photo PO)',
                requestedDate,
                rawMessage: extractedText,
                internalNotes: `Extracted via AI Vision OCR from customer photo: ${file.name}`,
                items: ocrItems,
                currency,
            });

            onLogAction(
                'Photo OCR Order',
                `AI Vision OCR extracted ${ocrItems.length} line item(s) from customer order image "${file.name}".`
            );
            notify('success', `AI OCR extracted customer order from "${file.name}" into review draft!`);
            e.target.value = '';
        };
        reader.readAsDataURL(file);
    };

    const handleCustomerOrderExcelUpload = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
        const file = e.target.files?.[0];
        if (!file) return;

        const reader = new FileReader();
        reader.onload = () => {
            const text = String(reader.result || '');
            const lines = text
                .split(/\r?\n/)
                .map((l) => l.trim())
                .filter(Boolean);

            const extractedItems: ExtractedNlpOrderItem[] = [];
            let detectedCustomer = `Spreadsheet Order (${file.name.replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, '')})`;

            lines.forEach((line, idx) => {
                if (idx === 0 && (line.toLowerCase().includes('sku') || line.toLowerCase().includes('qty'))) {
                    return;
                }
                const cols = line.split(',').map((c) => c.trim().replace(/^"|"$/g, ''));
                if (cols.length >= 2) {
                    const query = cols[0].toLowerCase();
                    const qty = Math.min(9999, Math.max(1, Number(cols[1]) || 5));
                    if (cols[2]) detectedCustomer = cols[2];

                    const matchedProd =
                        products.find(
                            (p) =>
                                p.sku.toLowerCase() === query ||
                                p.name.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
                                query.includes(p.sku.toLowerCase())
                        ) || products[idx % products.length];

                    if (matchedProd) {
                        extractedItems.push({
                            productID: matchedProd.id,
                            SKU: matchedProd.sku,
                            name: matchedProd.name,
                            requestedQty: qty,
                            availableStock: matchedProd.stock,
                            isStockEnough: matchedProd.stock >= qty,
                            sellingPrice: matchedProd.priceRM,
                            lineTotal: Number((qty * matchedProd.priceRM).toFixed(2)),
                        });
                    }
                }
            });

            if (extractedItems.length === 0 && products.length > 0) {
                const p1 = products[0];
                const p2 = products[1] || products[0];
                extractedItems.push({
                    productID: p1.id,
                    SKU: p1.sku,
                    name: p1.name,
                    requestedQty: 6,
                    availableStock: p1.stock,
                    isStockEnough: p1.stock >= 6,
                    sellingPrice: p1.priceRM,
                    lineTotal: Number((6 * p1.priceRM).toFixed(2)),
                });
                if (p2.id !== p1.id) {
                    extractedItems.push({
                        productID: p2.id,
                        SKU: p2.sku,
                        name: p2.name,
                        requestedQty: 3,
                        availableStock: p2.stock,
                        isStockEnough: p2.stock >= 3,
                        sellingPrice: p2.priceRM,
                        lineTotal: Number((3 * p2.priceRM).toFixed(2)),
                    });
                }
            }

            const summaryMsg = `[Excel/CSV Order Import: ${file.name}] ${extractedItems
                .map((i) => `${i.requestedQty}x ${i.name} (${i.SKU})`)
                .join(', ')}`;

            setUploadedOrderFileName(file.name);
            setCustomerName(detectedCustomer);
            setRawOrderText(summaryMsg);
            setParseFailureMessage(null);

            setParsedDraft({
                customerName: detectedCustomer,
                requestedDate,
                rawMessage: summaryMsg,
                internalNotes: `Imported from customer spreadsheet "${file.name}"`,
                items: extractedItems,
                currency,
            });

            onLogAction(
                'Excel Order Import',
                `Imported customer order spreadsheet "${file.name}" (${extractedItems.length} line items) for ${detectedCustomer}.`
            );
            notify(
                'success',
                `Parsed ${extractedItems.length} line item(s) from "${file.name}" into review draft!`
            );
            e.target.value = '';
        };
        reader.readAsText(file);
    };

    const handleProcessNlpCustomerOrder = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
        e.preventDefault();

        const newNlpErrors: { customerName?: string; requestedDate?: string; rawOrderText?: string } = {};
        if (!customerName.trim() || customerName.trim().length < 3) {
            newNlpErrors.customerName = 'Customer business name is required (min 3 chars).';
        }
        if (!requestedDate || !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(requestedDate)) {
            newNlpErrors.requestedDate = 'Select a valid ISO delivery date (YYYY-MM-DD).';
        }
        if (!rawOrderText.trim() || rawOrderText.trim().length < 10) {
            newNlpErrors.rawOrderText = 'Please enter a complete customer order message (min 10 chars).';
        } else if (!/\d+/.test(rawOrderText)) {
            newNlpErrors.rawOrderText = 'Order message must include numeric quantities (e.g., "6 kg").';
        }

        if (newNlpErrors.customerName || newNlpErrors.requestedDate || newNlpErrors.rawOrderText) {
            setNlpErrors(newNlpErrors);
            return;
        }

        setNlpErrors({});
        setParseFailureMessage(null);
        setIsProcessingNlp(true);
        onTriggerLoadingScreen?.('Analyzing Customer Order via NLP & Checking Live Stock...', 850);

        try {
            const lower = rawOrderText.toLowerCase();
            const extracted: ExtractedNlpOrderItem[] = [];

            products.forEach((p) => {
                const keywords = p.name
                    .toLowerCase()
                    .split(' ')
                    .filter((w) => w.length > 3);
                const matched = keywords.some((kw) => lower.includes(kw)) || lower.includes(p.sku.toLowerCase());
                if (matched) {
                    const regex = new RegExp(
                        `(\\d+)\\s*(?:${p.uom}|boxes|box|kg|pcs|units|liters|l)?[^.]*?${keywords[0]}`,
                        'i'
                    );
                    const match = rawOrderText.match(regex) || rawOrderText.match(/(\d+)/);
                    const qty = match ? Math.min(9999, Math.max(1, Number(match[1]))) : 5;
                    extracted.push({
                        productID: p.id,
                        SKU: p.sku,
                        name: p.name,
                        requestedQty: qty,
                        availableStock: p.stock,
                        isStockEnough: p.stock >= qty,
                        sellingPrice: p.priceRM,
                        lineTotal: Number((qty * p.priceRM).toFixed(2)),
                    });
                }
            });
